### [1] [Vakonomic Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18312)
*Ritoban Roy-Chowdhury,Mohammad Sina Nabizadeh,Oliver Gross,Anthony Gruber,Albert Chern*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于vakonomic变分原理的不可压缩欧拉方程离散化方法，该方法将流体视为体积保持微分同胚群上的测地线，通过离散Koopman表示和低秩Clebsch变量实现，能够精确保持Casimir不变量和开尔文环流定理，在低分辨率下仍具有稳定性和物理真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散化方法在处理Koopman表示带来的非完整约束时，缺乏变分处理的共识，导致离散流体轨迹不再保持测地线性质。

Method: 采用vakonomic变分原理替代传统的Lagrange-d'Alembert方法，将离散流体轨迹保持在(sub-)Riemannian流形上的测地线；利用低秩Clebsch变量构建高效动量映射表示。

Result: 所得vakonomic动力学是Lie-Poisson的，其解具有离散重标对称性，能够以机器精度满足Casimir不变量和离散开尔文环流定理；在低网格分辨率下仍表现稳定一致。

Conclusion: 该离散化方法提高了长期数值模拟的鲁棒性和物理真实性。

Abstract: We introduce a novel discretization of the incompressible Euler equations based on their interpretation as geodesic equations on the Lie group of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms. It is well known that encoding diffeomorphisms and their infinitesimal generators through a discretized Koopman representation places a nonholonomic constraint on discrete velocities, for which there is no consensus on a variational treatment. We show that taking the vakonomic perspective, as opposed to the usual perspective of Lagrange--d'Alembert, yields discrete fluid trajectories that remain geodesics on a (sub-)Riemannian manifold. In particular, the resulting vakonomic dynamics are Lie--Poisson and their solutions admit a discrete relabeling symmetry, leading to machine-precision satisfaction of Casimir invariants along with a discrete analogue of Kelvin's Circulation Theorem. Using an efficient momentum map representation based on low-rank Clebsch variables, we show that these vakonomic fluids behave stably and consistently even at low grid resolutions, leading to increased robustness and physical realism in the long term.

</details>


### [2] [Boundary Free Energies in Disordered Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18326)
*Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu,Mauris Chueng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无序伊辛模型中边界修正对自由能的影响，通过一维反例证明表面自由能密度可能依赖于范霍夫序列且随机修正可能不依概率收敛；在Dobrushin唯一性区域内证明自由能差在期望、几乎必然和L^p意义下的收敛性；推导了高斯边界耦合的精确插值恒等式；对缝翻转自由能差给出方差上界，并指出低温柔性高斯Edwards-Anderson问题仍待解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序伊辛模型中边界条件、归一化和有限体积序列对自由能边界修正的影响，澄清表面自由能密度的序列依赖性以及随机修正的收敛性问题。

Method: 通过一维i.i.d.反例论证表面自由能密度的非独立性；在Dobrushin唯一性区域内利用耦合方法证明收敛性；推导高斯边界耦合的精确有限体积插值恒等式；通过方差上界分析缝翻转自由能差。

Result: 一维反例表明表面自由能密度不必独立于范霍夫序列，随机修正不必依概率收敛；在d≥2的Dobrushin唯一性区域内，自由能差在期望、几乎必然和L^p意义下收敛；高斯边界耦合的插值恒等式不直接蕴含低温表面极限；缝翻转自由能差方差有4v|S_L|上界，一维例子表明对称性和有限矩不能确定刚度指数。

Conclusion: 边界条件对无序伊辛模型自由能的影响具有复杂的序列依赖性，高维Dobrushin唯一性区域内可建立收敛性，但低温柔性问题（特别是高斯Edwards-Anderson模型）仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: The boundary correction to the free energy of a disordered Ising model depends on the boundary condition, the normalization, and the finite-volume sequence. We give a one-dimensional i.i.d. counterexample showing that the surface free-energy density need not be independent of the van Hove sequence and that the corresponding random correction need not converge in probability. For bounded couplings in the Dobrushin uniqueness regime on cubic boxes in dimensions $d\geq2$, we prove convergence of the normalized free-to-fixed boundary free-energy difference in expectation, almost surely, and in every $L^p$, $1\leq p<\infty$. For Gaussian boundary couplings, we derive an exact finite-volume interpolation identity and explain why it does not by itself imply a low-temperature surface limit. For a seam-flip free-energy difference $D_L$ across a set $S_L$ of independent symmetric bonds of variance $v$, we prove $\Var(D_L)\leq4v\abs{S_L}$; one-dimensional examples show that symmetry and finite moments alone do not determine a stiffness exponent, and the low-temperature Gaussian Edwards--Anderson problem remains open.

</details>


### [3] [A converse to generalized Runcorn's theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18379)
*Vjekoslav Kovač,Ivica Smolić*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Runcorn定理的反问题（源于月球磁性的明显悖论），刻画了使特定积分方程对所有内外调和函数为零的平方可积复函数f的空间，并通过球谐展开和显式表示给出了解的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于月球磁性的一个明显悖论，旨在通过广义Runcorn定理的反问题来理解这一现象。Runcorn定理原本涉及地核与地幔之间的电磁感应，而月球也存在类似的磁场问题，需要更一般的数学框架来解释。

Method: 研究采用数学分析方法：首先在球壳区域{a≤|x|≤b}上考虑函数f与两个调和函数u、v梯度内积的积分恒为零的条件；然后根据半径a、b是否可变来分类解空间；最后通过球谐函数展开f，并给出显式表示。

Result: 得到了平方可积复函数f满足积分恒为零条件的完整刻画，解空间依赖于半径a和b是否被视为可变参数，并通过球谐展开给出了f的显式表示形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为广义Runcorn定理的反问题提供了完整的数学解答，其显式表示有助于理解月球磁场悖论背后的物理机制，并为相关逆问题研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study an inverse problem for generalized Runcorn's theorem motivated by an apparent paradox of lunar magnetism. In mathematical terms, we characterize square-integrable complex functions $f$ such that $$ \int_{\{ x\in\mathbb{R}^n : a \leq |x| \leq b \}} f(x) \nabla u(x)\cdot\nabla v(x)\,\mathrm{d}\mathrm{V}(x) = 0 $$ for every complex harmonic function $u$ in the inner ball $|x|<r_+$ and every complex harmonic function $v$ in the exterior region $|x|>r_-$ that vanishes at infinity. The solution space depends on whether the radii $a$ and $b$ such that $r_-<a<b<r_+$ are regarded as varying or fixed. The solutions are described through the expansion of $f$ into spherical harmonics, and explicit representations are provided.

</details>


### [4] [Tau functions and correlation functions of the bosonic universal character hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18780)
*Denghui Li,Jinzhou Liu,Zhaowen Yan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色通用特征（UC）层次结构的构造，基于带电荷自由玻色子框架探究其tau函数。对应李代数为$\mathfrak{\widehat{gl}}_{2\infty}$，证明tau函数可由有序指数算子序列表示，并推导出相关函数可表达为UC的乘积。值得注意的是，玻色UC层次的相关函数是广义相位模型相关函数的逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造玻色通用特征（UC）层次结构，并在带电荷自由玻色子框架下研究其性质，以拓展可积系统理论。

Method: 利用带电荷自由玻色子框架，通过有序指数算子序列表示tau函数，并推导相关函数与UC的乘积关系。李代数基础为$\mathfrak{\widehat{gl}}_{2\infty}$。

Result: 证明玻色UC层次结构的tau函数可由有序指数算子表示；相关函数可表达为UC的乘积；发现玻色UC层次的相关函数是广义相位模型相关函数的逆。

Conclusion: 成功构建了玻色UC层次结构，揭示了其与广义相位模型之间的对偶关系，为可积系统理论提供了新的结构。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the construction of the bosonic universal character (UC) hierarchy, whose tau functions are investigated within the framework of charged free bosons. The Lie algebra corresponding to the bosonic UC hierarchy is $\mathfrak{\widehat{gl}}_{2\infty}$. It is shown that the tau functions of bosonic UC hierarchy can be represented based on a series of ordered exponential operators. Furthermore, we derive correlation functions of the bosonic UC hierarchy, which can be expressed as the product of UCs. It is worth noting that the correlation functions of the bosonic UC hierarchy are the inverse of the correlation functions of the generalized phase model.

</details>


### [5] [Towards General Relativity as a generalized Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18837)
*Hartwig Winterroth,Lorenzo Fatibene*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用广义主丛理论发展了一种广义规范场论框架，旨在为Yang-Mills理论和广义相对论提供统一语言。通过定义广义主联络、建立运动学和动力学，提出了广义Yang-Mills理论，并证明向量束上的广义主联络等价于仿射联络（含焊接形式），从而在适当假设下恢复了真空广义相对论的Vielbein表述，暗示了一种广义规范引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将广义主丛理论应用于协变Lagrange场论，发展广义规范场论的具体实例，以期建立Yang-Mills理论与广义相对论的统一描述语言。

Method: 回顾Lie群纤维丛和广义主丛的基本定义；给出Lie群纤维丛联络和广义主联络的水平提升及局部刻画；在经典场论框架下研究广义主联络的运动学和动力学；证明向量束是广义主丛的特例，其上的广义主联络等价于由基本焊接形式给出的仿射联络。

Result: 提出了广义Yang-Mills理论的概念；在适当假设下，从该框架中恢复了真空广义相对论的Vielbein表述。

Conclusion: 该工作暗示了广义相对论可被视为一种广义规范引力理论，为统一描述Yang-Mills理论和引力提供了新视角。

Abstract: As an application of the generalized principal bundle theory to covariant Lagrangian field theories, we aim at the development of an instance of generalized gauge theories, with the prospect of a unifying language for Yang-Mills theories and General Relativity. After reviewing the basic definitions of Lie group fiber bundles and generalized principal bundles, we provide horizontal lift and local characterizations of Lie group fiber bundle connections and generalized principal connections. Subsequently, we consider in the framework of classical field theories the kinematics and dynamics sides of generalized principal connections, which lead to a proposed notion of generalized Yang-Mills theories. Finally, we show how vector bundles are examples of generalized principal bundles and that a generalized principal connection on a vector bundle is an affine connection given in terms of basic soldering forms. We are able to recover, under appropriate assumptions, the Vielbein formulation of (vacuum) General Relativity in this setting, hinting at a (generalized) gauge theory of gravity.

</details>


### [6] [Torsionful Killing-Yano Forms under T-Duality: Transformation Conditions and Emergent Isometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18842)
*Özgür Kelekçi,Ümit Ertem,Özgür Açık*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在非平凡三形式挠率时，Killing-Yano p-形式在阿贝尔T-对偶下的变换规律。通过使用含挠率的度量相容联络和Buscher规则，推导了紧凑、无坐标的变换定律，并应用于S^3的Hopf T-对偶、Schwarzschild时空和Nappi-Witten平面波等例子，最后给出了广义几何和双场理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究存在非平凡三形式挠率时，Killing-Yano形式在阿贝尔T-对偶下的变换行为，填补该领域理论空白。

Method: 利用含挠率的度量相容联络和Buscher规则，推导Killing-Yano p-形式在阿贝尔T-对偶下的坐标无关变换定律；通过显式匹配假设，证明Killing 1-形式在对偶映射下的保持条件（横向分量与等度规方向无关，对偶圆分量由标量修正方程确定）。

Result: 推导了Killing-Yano形式在含挠率情况下的T-对偶变换定律；给出了Killing 1-形式在对偶下保持的精确条件；在Hopf T-对偶S^3、Schwarzschild时空和Nappi-Witten平面波中显式验证了变换后的Killing-Yano形式。

Conclusion: Killing-Yano形式在含挠率阿贝尔T-对偶下具有明确的变换规律；对偶几何中的涌现Killing 1-形式可由原始对偶标架中的广义Killing数据导出，为广义几何和双场理论提供了新的理解视角。

Abstract: We investigate the transformation of Killing-Yano (KY) $p$-forms under Abelian T-duality in the presence of a non-trivial three-form torsion. Using a torsionful, metric-compatible connection and the Buscher rules of T-duality, we derive compact, coordinate-free transformation laws applicable to KY forms of arbitrary degree under explicit matching assumptions. In particular, we show that a Killing 1-form is preserved by the direct duality map whenever its transverse components are independent of the isometry direction, with the dual circle component determined by a scalar correction equation. The framework is then applied to several examples, including the Hopf T-dual of $S^3$, Schwarzschild spacetime, and the Nappi-Witten plane wave with exact NS-NS torsion, where we analyze the transformed Killing-Yano forms explicitly. Finally, we give a generalized-geometric and double-field-theoretic interpretation of emergent Killing 1-forms in the T-dual geometry, showing how they arise from generalized Killing data in the original duality frame.

</details>


### [7] [Imaging through rough interfaces: The shower curtain effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18947)
*Cristophe Gomez,Knut Sølna*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将散射层建模为粗糙界面而非随机介质，分析了“淋浴帘效应”中成像分辨率和信噪比与粗糙界面统计特性及相对位置的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对“淋浴帘效应”的分析主要将散射层建模为随机介质，但许多实际物理配置中散射层更自然地表现为粗糙界面，因此需要新的理论框架来更准确地描述这一现象。

Method: 将散射层建模为粗糙界面，推导出成像分辨率和信噪比的显式表达式，并分析这些量如何依赖于粗糙界面的统计特性及其在物体与观察者之间的相对位置。

Result: 获得了成像分辨率和信噪比与粗糙界面统计特性及相对位置之间的定量依赖关系。

Conclusion: 粗糙界面模型为“淋浴帘效应”提供了新的理论分析框架，能够更准确地描述散射层在不同位置时的成像质量变化规律。

Abstract: The quality of an image observed through a scattering layer, such as a shower curtain, depends strongly on the relative position of the scattering layer between the object and the observer. This well-known phenomenon is commonly referred to as the shower curtain effect. When the scattering layer is placed close to the observer, the image is strongly degraded, whereas if it is located close to the object, the object may still be observed with relatively high resolution. Previous analyses of the shower curtain effect have primarily modeled the scattering layer as a section of a random medium. In this work, we present a new analysis in which the scattering layer is modeled instead as a rough interface, a description that arises naturally in many physical configurations. Within this framework, we derive explicit characterizations of both the image resolution and the signal-to-noise ratio, and determine how these quantities depend on the statistical properties of the rough interface and on its relative location between the object and the observer.

</details>


### [8] [Integrable multi-species SSEP with reactive particle species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18959)
*Mathieu Dabrowski,Loïc Poulain d'Andecy,Eric Ragoucy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由Yang-Baxter方程集合论解构造的可积排斥马尔可夫过程，推广了多物种SSEP。首先引入含反应性粒子的(p,1)-SSEP模型，证明了周期和开边界条件下的可积性；接着推广到含任意数量反应性粒子物种的(p,q)-SSEP，证明了周期可积性并引入可积边界；最后计算了开边界(p,q)-SSEP非平衡稳态中的相关物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多物种SSEP推广到包含反应性粒子物种的情形，利用Yang-Baxter方程的集合论解构造更一般的可积排斥过程，并研究其边界可积性和非平衡稳态物理量。

Method: 利用Yang-Baxter方程集合论解构造可积马尔可夫过程；通过反射方程的Baxterisation引入可积边界条件；采用组合论方法研究周期扇区结构；利用可积性方法计算非平衡稳态物理量。

Result: 建立了(p,1)-SSEP周期情况的完整扇区组合研究；证明了(p,1)-SSEP在周期和两种开边界下的可积性；证明了(p,q)-SSEP在周期情况下的可积性并引入推广的可积边界；对于一种可积边界，计算了开边界(p,q)-SSEP非平衡稳态的物理量。

Conclusion: 成功构造了含反应性粒子物种的可积排斥过程，并严格证明了其可积性，为非平衡统计物理提供了新的可解模型。

Abstract: We investigate integrable exclusion Markov processes constructed from set-theoretical solutions of the Yang-Baxter Equation (YBE) that generalise the multi-species Symmetric Simple Exclusion Process (SSEP). We first introduce the process called the $ (p,1) $-SSEP that is analogous to the multi-species SSEP but with an extra particle species qualified as reactive. Reactive species are able to evaporate and condensate by pairs or to transform by pairs depending on the interpretation. We provide a full combinatorial study of the sectors in the periodic case. We prove the integrability of the process in the periodic case and in the open case for two types of boundaries that we introduce using Baxterisations of solutions of the reflection equation. Next we move on to the process called $ (p,q) $-SSEP, i.e. the multi-species SSEP with an arbitrary number of reactive particle species. We prove its integrability in the periodic case and, for the open case, we introduce integrable boundaries generalising the ones considered for the $ (p,1) $-SSEP. Finally, we define physical quantities relevant to the models and we compute them in the non-equilibrium stationary state of the open $ (p,q) $-SSEP for one type of integrable boundaries.

</details>


### [9] [Topological Foundations of Multi-Field Instabilities in Continua: Part 1: Foundations Part 2:Analytical Formulation for 1-D Spin Chains Part 3: Numerical Upscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18995)
*Klaus Regenauer-Lieb,Francois Nicot,Amir Saker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过三部分研究，建立了一个无参数、拓扑分类的颗粒介质多场失稳框架，将麦克斯韦刚性计数扩展到动态非平衡过程。核心发现是基于奇偶通道数的对称性破缺：奇数通道（N为奇数）产生零模式，导致持续性构型漂移；偶数通道则表现为谐波约束。通过离散VMC接触公式、奇偶定理、1D自旋链解析解和数值上尺度化验证了这一理论，并提出了可测量的DEM观测量和可验证的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典麦克斯韦刚性计数理论从静态平衡系统推广到动态非平衡颗粒介质，建立参数无关的拓扑分类框架，以解释和预测多场耦合失稳行为，特别是时间反演对称性破缺导致的漂移现象。

Method: 三部分结构：(1) 建立离散VMC接触公式，提出奇偶定理；(2) 1-D自旋链解析求解N=3（漂移）和N=4（谐波约束）；(3) 四精度数值积分三对角斜对称Onsager链（N=3至50），验证奇偶性差异。

Result: 奇数N产生持续构型漂移，偶数N表现为谐波约束；偶数链频率标度律|λ_min^even|~γπ/N；VMC通道可映射为可测量的DEM观测量。

Conclusion: 建立了颗粒介质中基于拓扑奇偶性的多场失稳分类框架，提供了参数无关的预测能力和四个可证伪的固结仪实验方案。

Abstract: This three-part series establishes a parameter-free, topological classification of multi-field instability in granular continua, extending Maxwell's rigidity count to dynamic, non-equilibrium processes.
  Part 1 (Foundations): a discrete Volumetric-Mechanical-Configurational (VMC) contact formulation maps contact-scale topology to macroscopic multiphysics coupling. A Parity Theorem, $\det(\mathsf{L})=(-1)^N\det(\mathsf{L})$, forces a structural null-mode for every odd channel count $N$, creating "Gateway" layers of broken time-reversal symmetry; once the basis-invariant Gateway number $\mathcal{G}_{\rm inv}\geq 1$, gyroscopic pumping drives non-modal transient amplification along the null direction.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过分析候选度量η₀=Δ†Δ的正定性及非强制性，构造C_c^∞(0,∞)在范数‖ψ‖_{η₀}=‖Δψ‖下的完备化，研究其酉等价结构、自伴实现及谱性质。分析候选本征函数Δψ_z=x^{-z}在完备空间中的归属问题。

Result: 1) 完备化空间与L²(ℝ₊)酉等价，自由自伴实现为膨胀生成算子，谱为纯绝对连续谱ℝ。2) 有界夹积Δ†h(D)Δ无有界可逆元。3) 传输对称算子的亏指数为(∞,∞)，其伴随以每个实点为无穷重特征值，自由扩展为纯连续谱。4) 对于Re z=1/2，候选本征函数Δψ_z不属于完备空间。

Conclusion: 在L²基度量完备化框架下，BBM的原始机制无法产生黎曼零点对应的点谱态。这一结论与实现方式无关，对BBM问题及更广泛的非厄米谱理论具有重要启示。

Abstract: The Bender--Brody--Müller (BBM) Hamiltonian was proposed as a non-Hermitian Hilbert--Pólya operator. We analyze the Hilbert completion induced, on the standard half-line $L^2$ core, by BBM's candidate metric $\hatη=\sin^2(\hat p/2)=Δ^\daggerΔ/4$. The form $η_0=Δ^\daggerΔ$ is positive with trivial kernel but is not coercive. Completing $C_c^\infty(0,\infty)$ in the norm $\|ψ\|_{η_0}=\|Δψ\|$ gives a Hilbert space canonically unitarily equivalent to $L^2(\mathbb R_+)$. Its free self-adjoint realization is the dilation generator, with simple, purely absolutely continuous spectrum $\mathbb R$. The analysis yields two spectral statements of interest beyond the BBM problem. First, no bounded sandwich $Δ^\dagger h(D)Δ$ is boundedly invertible. Second, the transported symmetric operator has deficiency indices $(\infty,\infty)$ and an adjoint with every real point as an eigenvalue of infinite multiplicity, while its free extension is purely continuous. The realization-independent BBM conclusion concerns the candidate eigenfunctions: $Δψ_z=x^{-z}$, so for $\operatorname{Re}z=1/2$ they do not belong to the completed space. Thus the original BBM boundary-condition/eigenfunction mechanism cannot produce point-spectrum Riemann-zero states in this $L^2$-based metric completion.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将问题建模为具有两个自由度的自治Lagrange系统，系统仅含曲率κ和质量比μ两个参数。针对κ>0的情况，采用解析方法与计算机辅助证明相结合的手段进行分析。

Result: 建立了正曲率情形下的若干严格分类结果，为先前仅有数值证据的现象提供了严格证明；发现正曲率对三角平衡点L₄和L₅具有稳定化效应，而负曲率则呈现相反的效果；给出了正曲率与平面及负曲率情形下平衡点行为定性差异的拓扑解释。

Conclusion: 正曲率对三角平衡点具有稳定化作用，负曲率则相反；研究聚焦小质量比μ情形，不同曲率符号导致平衡点行为的定性差异可通过拓扑结构加以解释。

Abstract: We continue the study initiated by Kilin ({\em Reg. Chaot. Dyn.} 4, (1999)) and by Martínez and Simó ({\em Celest. Mech. Dynam. Astronom.} 128, (2017)) on the classification and stability of the relative equilibria of the restricted three-body problem in two-dimensional spaces of constant curvature, which generalize the classical Lagrange points of the planar problem. After formulating the problem as an autonomous Lagrangian system with two degrees of freedom, whose only parameters are the curvature $κ$ and the mass ratio $μ$ of the primaries, we establish several classification results for the case $κ>0$ by combining analytical methods with computer-assisted proofs. These results provide rigorous confirmation of phenomena for which previously only numerical evidence was available. We also provide topological explanations for the qualitative differences between the behavior of relative equilibria in positive curvature and that observed in the planar and negative-curvature cases. Our analysis focuses on the regime of small $μ$ and indicates that positive curvature has a stabilizing effect on the triangular equilibria $\Ll_4$ and $\Ll_5$, whereas negative curvature appears to have the opposite effect.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展先前关于两自旋粒子标量和赝标量一阶积分的分类工作，系统研究自旋-轨道、自旋-自旋、张量及二次自旋-轨道耦合项共存时的一阶矢量积分，填补该领域分类空白。

Conclusion: 实现了含自旋-轨道、自旋-自旋、张量及二次自旋-轨道耦合的两粒子系统一阶矢量积分完备分类，拓展了量子超可积性的已知结构。

Abstract: We study quantum superintegrability for two interacting non-relativistic spin-$\frac12$ particles in three-dimensional Euclidean space. The Hamiltonian contains a central potential together with spin-orbit, spin-spin, tensor, and quadratic spin-orbit interaction terms, all depending only on the relative distance. We restrict the classification to the $V_4=0$ case, so that the spin-momentum interaction term is not included. We determine all such systems admitting non-trivial first-order vector integrals of motion. For this purpose, we construct the most general Hermitian first-order vector operator built from the relative position, momentum, orbital angular momentum, and the two spin vectors. The commutativity condition with the Hamiltonian leads to an overdetermined system of radial determining equations, whose solution gives the complete list of admissible potentials and the corresponding vector integrals within this class. The results extend the previous classifications of scalar and pseudo-scalar first-order integrals for two particles with spin. We also discuss selected symmetry algebras generated by the vector integrals and show, in one representative case, how a scalar reduction leads to exact Coulomb- and oscillator-type solutions.

</details>

*Yuri Taglieri Sáo,Geraldo de Freitas Maciel,Julian Cardoso Eleutério*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成不确定性量化和全局敏感性分析的概率方法论，用于尾矿坝溃坝分析，通过降维和元建模处理高维输出和计算成本问题，并在复杂地形基准案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尾矿坝溃坝分析对洪水灾害评估、应急规划和风险估计至关重要，但溃坝发展、释放体积和尾矿流变学的不确定性严重影响分析结果，现有方法缺乏对不确定性的系统量化。

Method: 提出模块化非侵入式概率框架，集成不确定性量化与全局敏感性分析；采用降维和元建模（metamodeling）处理高维空间地图输出和确定性模拟计算成本问题；在复杂地形基准案例中使用HEC-RAS v6.6进行演示，考虑溃坝参数和流变特性的不确定性。

Result: 量化了最大流深和到达时间的不确定性，表征了其统计分布；通过敏感性图识别了主要输入变量的空间影响：溃坝参数在坝附近占主导，而屈服应力在下游更远处起主导作用。

Conclusion: 该模块化非侵入式框架可与其他确定性模型耦合，适用于不同溃坝场景，支持更标准化和基于风险的评估。

Abstract: Tailings dam-breach analyses are essential for flood-hazard assessment, emergency planning and risk estimation, but their results are strongly affected by uncertainties in breach development, released volume and tailings rheology. This study proposes an efficient probabilistic methodology that integrates uncertainty quantification and global sensitivity analysis for tailings dam-breach studies. High-dimensional outputs (spatial maps) and the computational cost of deterministic simulations are addressed through dimensionality reduction and metamodeling. The methodology is demonstrated on a benchmark case with complex terrain using HEC-RAS v6.6 and considering uncertainties in breach parameters and rheological properties. The results quantify uncertainty in maximum flow depth and arrival time, characterize their statistical distributions and identify the spatial influence of the main input variables through sensitivity maps. Sensitivity indices reveal the dominance of breach parameters near the dam and yield stress farther downstream. The modular and non-intrusive framework can be coupled with other deterministic models and applied to different dam-breach scenarios, supporting more standardized and risk-informed assessments.

</details>

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种嵌套运动动力学框架，结合可行性引导的树搜索和大语言模型接触计划采样，用于人形机器人快速规划未知场景中的新颖行为，并通过强化学习控制器实现真实世界的执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人 locomotion-manipulation 具有高维、混合和欠驱动特性，导致在新场景中快速规划新颖行为仍然是一个基本挑战。

Method: 提出嵌套运动动力学框架用于快速可行性检查与动力学一致轨迹生成；结合可行性引导的树搜索和LLM的接触计划采样策略；使用RL控制器跟踪生成的轨迹。

Result: 该框架显著改进了搜索过程，生成的轨迹质量足够高，可在真实世界的人形locomotion-manipulation场景中执行。

Conclusion: 嵌套运动动力学框架结合LLM采样和RL控制能够有效解决人形机器人在未知场景中快速规划新颖行为的问题。

Abstract: Fast planning of novel behaviors in unseen scenarios remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. The high-dimensional, hybrid, and underactuated nature of humanoid loco-manipulation continues to hinder the realization of this goal. In this paper, we address this challenge by proposing a nested kino-dynamic framework for rapid feasibility checking and dynamically consistent trajectory generation given a candidate contact sequence. By integrating this module with a feasibility-guided tree search and a Large Language Model (LLM)-based contact plan sampling strategy, we demonstrate that the proposed framework can substantially improve the search process. Furthermore, we show that the generated trajectories can be tracked using a reinforcement learning (RL)-based controller and show that the resulting trajectories are of sufficiently high quality for execution in real-world loco-manipulation scenarios. A supplementary video is available at: https://youtu.be/R6qCHoCormQ.

</details>

*Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需速度估计的强化学习扭矩控制框架，应用于重型高扭矩四足机器人（Unitree B1），在Isaac Sim仿真中实现了3.5 m/s线速度和1.5 rad/s角速度，并能无需外部传感器自主上下楼梯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习 locomotion 框架多为位置控制，对地形适应性差且需要状态估计（如线速度）；同时大多使用小型轻量四足机器人，硬件限制使其难以完成高复杂度任务。本文旨在解决这些问题，探索适用于重型高扭矩四足机器人的强化学习扭矩控制框架。

Method: 采用强化学习扭矩控制框架，无需智能体当前速度信息即可跟踪期望线速度；使用Nvidia Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab仿真平台，在Unitree B1四足机器人上进行训练和验证。

Result: 在仿真中，Unitree B1四足机器人达到3.5 m/s线速度和1.5 rad/s角速度；无需外部传感器即可自主上下楼梯；能穿越崎岖地形并有效跟踪期望线速度。

Conclusion: 本文证明无需速度估计的强化学习扭矩控制框架可在重型高扭矩四足机器人上实现高性能 locomotion，减少了对状态估计和外部传感器的依赖，拓展了四足机器人在复杂任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for legged robots is advancing locomotion, demonstrating its ability to adapt to new and challenging terrain. Traditionally, these RL locomotion frameworks are position-based, making the policy less adaptable to terrain types and requiring state estimation techniques in the observation space, i.e., linear velocity. Moreover, these RL frameworks often use small, lightweight quadrupeds that are limited in their viability for high-complexity tasks due to hardware constraints. This work explores an RL torque control framework for heavyweight high-torque quadrupeds. The RL framework in this paper can traverse rough terrain and effectively track a desired linear velocity without requiring knowledge of the agent's current velocity. Using Nvidia's Isaac Sim and Isaac Lab, simulation results of the RL torque control policy are shown on the Unitree B1 quadruped, achieving speeds of 3.5 m/s and 1.5 rad/s. In addition, the quadruped can walk up and down stairs without the aid of an exteroceptive sensor.

</details>


### [17] [The Open Ant: A Robot Platform for Reinforcement Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18488)
*Elena Sorina Lupu,Patrick Spieler,Khurram Javed,Kris De Asis,John D. Martin,Martha Steenstrup,Joseph Modayil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Open Ant，一个物理版本的Gymnasium Ant环境，能够在大约一小时内直接从真实机器人经验中学习行走策略，支持SARSA(λ)和SAC两种算法，并实现了仿真到现实的策略迁移，硬件和软件均开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习研究主要基于仿真环境，但仿真到现实的迁移存在不确定性，缺乏一个能够简化从仿真到物理机器人过渡的物理平台。

Method: 设计并构建了Open Ant物理平台（Gymnasium Ant的实体版本），配备对应仿真环境；在实体机器人上直接训练SARSA(λ)和SAC算法，同时进行仿真到现实的策略迁移实验；通过新用户上手速度和硬件维修便捷性评估平台生态支持能力。

Result: 从零开始在物理机器人上约一小时内学会行走策略；两种不同RL算法（SARSA(λ)和SAC）均成功；仿真训练的策略可迁移到现实；新用户能快速上手且平台易于维修更新。

Conclusion: Open Ant平台能有效弥合仿真与物理现实之间的鸿沟，建议RL研究人员在仿真环境之外使用该平台，以便在评估中更便捷地纳入真实机器人实验。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has demonstrated success in both physical and simulated domains; however, the predominant methodology remains rooted in simulations. The predominance of simulations makes translating research to physical reality uncertain for both algorithms and researchers. We propose a physical platform that is designed to simplify the transition. In this paper, we present the Open Ant: a physical variant of the commonly used Gymnasium Ant environment, along with a simulation. We demonstrate that competent walking policies can be learned from scratch in approximately one hour directly from the physical robot's experience for two substantially different RL algorithms: SARSA($λ$) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). Separately, we show policies that were learned in simulation transfer to reality. We also examine how well the platform supports a nimble experimental ecosystem. Specifically, we observe the speed with which new users from diverse backgrounds achieve their first success with the platform, and how easily the platform can be repaired and updated when hardware issues arise. Both the hardware design and software are available as open-source on GitHub for ease of customization. In summary, we advocate for the use of the Open Ant for RL researchers who frequently use simulated environments, so they can more easily include robot experiments in their evaluations.

</details>

*Himanshu Gupta,Kelvin Aladum,Nisar Ahmed,Bradley Hayes,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在拥挤环境中机器人拦截移动目标的问题，将其建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），并对比了顺序路径-速度规划器与统一转向-速度规划器在树搜索中的表现。实验表明，在高密度人群中（最多200人），统一规划器的安全拦截率比顺序规划器高31个百分点，所需时间少44%，揭示了空间限制的结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拥挤环境中拦截移动目标时，人类导航意图不可直接观察，机器人必须推理多种可能的未来交互结果。现有方法在低密度场景表现尚可，但高密度场景下的性能差异及其根本原因尚不明确，特别是动作空间结构对搜索树和计算资源分配的影响有待探究。

Method: 将拦截问题形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），在固定计算预算下通过树搜索在线求解。对比两种动作空间结构：顺序路径-速度规划器（先规划空间路径，再沿路径调节速度）与统一转向-速度规划器（在树搜索中联合分支转向和速度决策）。在最多200人的仿真环境中进行控制变量实验。

Result: 在低人群密度下两种方法表现相似；随着密度增加，差异显著扩大。最高密度时，顺序规划器的安全拦截率比统一规划器低31个百分点，所需时间多44%。

Conclusion: 顺序路径-速度规划存在结构性缺陷：空间路径的预先确定限制了后续速度调节的灵活性，导致高密度场景下性能骤降。统一转向-速度联合规划在高密度人群中具有显著优势，是更优的动作空间设计选择。

Abstract: Target interception in crowded environments requires reaching a moving objective while navigating among multiple uncertain human agents. Since human navigation intent is not directly observable, the robot must reason over multiple possible future interaction outcomes. We formulate interception in crowds as a partially observable Markov decision process and solve it online using tree search under a fixed computational budget. In this setting, the action-space structure directly shapes the search tree and how computational effort is allocated. We perform a controlled comparison between a sequential path-speed planner, which first plans a spatial path and then modulates speed along it, and a unified planner that jointly branches over steering and speed within tree search. Across simulations with up to 200 humans, both approaches perform similarly at low crowd density but diverge sharply as density increases. At the highest crowd density, the sequential planner has a safe-interception rate 31 percentage points lower and requires 44% more time than the unified steering-speed planner, revealing a structural limitation of spatial restriction. Project webpage: https://tic-planning.github.io/

</details>

*Ryan Gomes Paiva,Conrad Ho,Jiarong Kang,Kunzhao Ren,Xiangru Xu,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DASH（Ducted Aerial Spring Hopper）的微型空地两栖机器人，通过将涵道风扇同轴机体与弹性腿有机集成，实现了飞行与高效地面弹跳的双模式运动，并采用接触隐式模型预测控制器自动选择运动模式，在障碍穿越任务中验证了自主模式切换能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空地两栖机器人往往机械结构复杂，在统一平台上同时实现空中与地面运动时，难以兼顾机械简约性、运动多样性和能量效率。本文旨在设计一种机械极简、运动灵活且能效高的空地两栖机器人。

Method: 采用涵道风扇同轴机体作为主推进单元提供推力矢量控制实现敏捷飞行；在底部集成轻量弹簧腿，与涵道风扇配合通过能量循环实现高效地面弹跳；使用接触隐式模型预测控制器（contact-implicit MPC）自动优化选择运动模式与驱动方式。

Result: 成功验证了DASH在周期性弹跳、空中飞行以及无需显式模式切换的自主障碍穿越等任务中的性能，证明了设计和控制方案的有效性。

Conclusion: DASH通过涵道风扇与弹簧腿的有机融合，配合接触隐式MPC控制器，实现了机械极简、运动灵活且能效高的空地两栖运动，为未来微型两栖机器人的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a novel and minimalistic design of an aerial-terrestrial robot DASH: Ducted Aerial Spring Hopper. The goal is to enable both aerial and ground locomotion capabilities on a unified mobile robot that is mechanically-minimalistic, locomotion-versatile, and energy-efficient. We propose an organic integration of ducted fan co-axial body with a springy leg at the bottom for realization. The ducted fan module provides thrust-vectoring as the main actuation for agile flying; when it is combined with the light-weight spring leg, the robot realizes highly efficient ground hopping with energy circulation. Moreover, to realize optimal locomotion with two modes, we employ a contact-implicit model predictive controller to automatically choose locomotion modes and actuation. We successfully validated the design and control of DASH through a range of tasks, including periodic hopping, aerial flight, and mode-free locomotion with autonomous mode transitions during obstacle traversal.

</details>


### [20] [Two-Stage Extrinsic Calibration of a Static Line-Scanning Lidar with a Rotary Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18578)
*Vikram Shree,Hike Danakian,Long Nguyen,Rajanish Gokidi,Patrick Nercessian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究如何标定旋转平台转轴与线扫描激光雷达坐标系之间的变换关系，以提升3D点云重建质量，并提出了静态和动态两种自动估计方法，在真实数据集上验证了其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用旋转平台配合线扫描激光雷达进行3D扫描时，转轴与激光雷达坐标系的变换关系未知且难以精确获取，而任何标定误差都会直接影响点云重建质量，导致物体表示失真。因此需要自动、精确地标定该变换。

Method: 提出了静态和动态两种自动估计旋转平台转轴变换的方法，并在自制的旋转平台与FMCW激光雷达上采集真实数据集进行验证，研究了不同初始条件下的收敛特性。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上验证了所提算法，并分析了多种初始条件下的收敛特性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动标定方法能够有效估计旋转平台转轴相对于线扫描激光雷达的变换关系，从而提升3D点云重建的精度。

Abstract: A line-scanning lidar yields range and azimuth values in a fixed plane. To perceive surrounding objects in 3D, there must be relative motion between the lidar plane and the object. Thus, using a rotating base-platform is promising for industrial applications where objects need to be scanned or inspected precisely, and is the main focus of this work. In the rotary platform setup, a 3D point cloud of an object can be constructed if the axis of rotation and the precise motion about that axis are known. However, this setup gives rise to the following problem: how can the axis of rotation of the platform be accurately identified with respect to the lidar coordinate system? It is referred to as the calibration problem in the robotics community. Any inaccuracy in this transformation directly affects the quality of the reconstructed point cloud, leading to misrepresentation of the object of interest. In this work, we explore automated approaches to statically and dynamically estimate the transformation of a rotary platform's axis of rotation with respect to a static line-scanning lidar. The proposed algorithms have been validated on real-world datasets obtained from a custom made rotary platform and an FMCW lidar, and their convergence characteristics are studied for various initial conditions.

</details>


### [21] [STeP: Signal Temporal Logic for Precise Specifications for Action Generation with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18580)
*Kasra Torshizi,Anukriti Singh,Sidharth Mathur,Khuzema Habib,Leo Du,Pratap Tokekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出层次化框架，以信号时序逻辑（STL）作为高层语言理解与低层机器人执行的共享表征，将自然语言指令分解为子任务并生成STL规范，结合STL引导的模型预测控制或学习策略监控执行，实现可解释、可验证的语言条件机器人规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽泛化能力强，但缺乏可解释性，且在遵循包含空间、时间、逻辑约束的精确自然语言指令时表现不佳。

Method: 构建层次化框架：高层策略使用VLM将语言指令分解为子任务，为每个子任务生成STL规范并选择低层策略；低层通过STL引导的MPC直接执行约束，或对感知复杂/接触密集行为采用STL监控学习策略；利用STL进行计划验证、子任务监控与重规划。

Result: 在真实桌面场景上验证，表明形式化规范能够提升语言条件机器人规划的精度、可靠性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 将STL作为语言意图与机器人执行之间的形式化桥梁，可有效提升基于自然语言的机器人规划的形式化验证能力与运行时鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown impressive generalization, but often lack interpretability and can struggle to follow precise natural language instructions that encode spatial, temporal, and logical requirements. We propose a hierarchical framework that uses Signal Temporal Logic (STL) as a shared representation connecting high-level language understanding with low-level robot execution. A high-level policy leverages a VLM to decompose language instructions into high-level subtasks, generate STL specifications for each subtask, and choose a low-level policy for executing each subtask. The STL specifications translate language-derived intent into precise constraints, and the low-level policy selection determines whether those constraints are enforced directly through STL-guided model-predictive control or monitored during execution of a learned policy for perceptually complex, or contact-rich behaviors. By integrating STL into plan validation, low-level policy, subtask monitoring, and replanning, our framework enables language-derived plans to be checked, optimized, and revised at runtime using a common formal structure. We evaluate the approach on a real-world tabletop domain, demonstrating how formal specifications can improve the precision, reliability, and interpretability of language-conditioned robot planning.

</details>


### [22] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap under Real-Time Constraints in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18586)
*Hossein Maghsoumi,Yaser P. Fallah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地将自动驾驶赛车的 sim-to-real 迁移问题框架化为一个全堆栈、实时系统问题，提出了三层视角（物理/计算/执行）分析跨层失配传播机制，并给出了诊断指标、缓解策略与可重复基准测试指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车在高速、紧稳定性边界和强实时约束的极端工况下，sim-to-real 鸿沟尤为严重；模拟中表现良好的控制器迁移到物理平台后性能急剧下降，其根源在于动力学失配、估计延迟和执行延迟等多层因素的交互影响，而现有工作缺乏系统性的跨层分析框架。

Method: 本文提出一个结构化三层视角（Physical/Cyber/Execution）来分析失配在闭环反馈中的传播与放大机制；引入超越标准单圈时间的诊断指标集（性能翻转、稳定性度量、对延迟和噪声的敏感性、延迟分布特征）；从部署导向视角综合缓解策略，强调执行感知与延迟感知设计；提出在计算和时序约束下可重复、公平评估的基准测试指南。

Result: 形成了跨层失效机制的分析框架，明确了物理层、计算层和执行层之间失配如何交互放大，并提供了可操作的设计原则用于部署逼近动力学极限的自动驾驶赛车系统。

Conclusion: Sim-to-real 迁移在极端工况下本质上是一个全栈实时系统问题，必须从跨层视角进行系统分析；所提出的框架和指南可为实际部署的自动驾驶赛车系统提供有效设计原则。

Abstract: Autonomous racing exposes the sim-to-real gap under extreme operating conditions characterized by high speed, tight stability margins, and stringent real-time constraints. Although simulation is indispensable for development, controllers that perform well in simulation often degrade abruptly on physical platforms due to interacting effects of dynamics mismatch, estimation delay, and execution-layer latency. This paper frames sim-to-real transfer in autonomous racing as a full-stack, real-time systems problem. We introduce a structured three-layer perspective (Physical/Cyber/Execution) to analyze how mismatches propagate and amplify through closed-loop feedback. We present diagnostic metrics beyond nominal lap time, including performance flip, stability-oriented measures, sensitivity to delay and noise, and latency distribution characterization. Mitigation strategies are synthesized from a deployment-oriented viewpoint, emphasizing execution-aware and delay-aware design. Finally, we outline benchmarking guidelines that enable reproducible and fair sim-to-real evaluation under compute and timing constraints. The resulting framework clarifies cross-layer failure mechanisms and provides practical design principles for deployable autonomous racing systems operating near dynamic limits.

</details>


### [23] [Intelligent Multi-UAV Navigation in ITNTNs: A Hierarchical LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18604)
*Zijiang Yan,Hao Zhou,Wael Jaafar,Jianhua Pei,Ping Wang,Halim Yanikomeroglu,Hina Tabassum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层LLM驱动控制框架，云级LLM在HAPS上负责慢时间尺度的全局负载均衡，轻量级边缘LLM在UAV上将局部观测转化为战术子目标，再由快速物理DRL控制器执行无碰撞、感知切换的轨迹，仿真表明能显著降低碰撞率并提升系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速无人机在3D空中高速公路部署需要协调物理飞行运动学和多层网络切换，但DRL缺乏零样本战略推理能力，LLM推理延迟高不适用于实时气动控制，因此需要一种结合两者优势的分层架构。

Method: 提出分层LLM驱动控制框架：云级LLM部署在HAPS上负责慢时间尺度全局负载均衡；轻量级边缘LLM部署在单个UAV上，将局部观测转化为战术子目标；子目标指导快速时间尺度的物理DRL控制器执行无碰撞、感知切换的轨迹。

Result: 相比现有基线方法，该智能体架构显著降低了碰撞率，并提高了系统总吞吐量。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层架构有效结合了LLM的战略推理能力和DRL的快速控制能力，解决了高速无人机在三维空中高速公路上的协调控制问题。

Abstract: The deployment of high-speed Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) in 3D aerial highways necessitates robust coordination of physical flight kinematics and multi-tier network handovers. While Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers rapid tactical control, it lacks the zero-shot strategic reasoning required to quickly adapt to dynamic Integrated Terrestrial and Non-Terrestrial Networks (ITNTNs). Conversely, Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at semantic reasoning but suffer from high inference latency, rendering them unsuitable for real-time aerodynamic control. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel Hierarchical LLM-driven control framework. A massive cloud-based LLM deployed on a High-Altitude Platform Station (HAPS) manages slow-timescale global load balancing, while lightweight edge-LLMs on individual UAVs translate local observations into tactical sub-goals. These sub-goals guide a fast-timescale physical DRL controller to execute collision-free, handover-aware trajectories. Simulation results demonstrate that our agentic architecture significantly reduces collision rates and improves aggregate system throughput compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [24] [On the Limits of Sampling-Based Reachability: Geometry, Dynamics, and Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18606)
*Jixian Liu,Ihab Tabbara,Hussein Sibai,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究基于采样的可达集恢复问题，将其建模为几何支撑估计。证明了在初始集补集的正可达性和动力系统的Lipschitz连续性条件下，概率质量覆盖保证可提升为Hausdorff距离精度r，给出了样本复杂度上界Õ((e^{3LT}/r)^n)，并证明了指数依赖于维度和时间horizon的本质不可避免性（下界Ω((e^{LT}/r)^n)）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可达性分析在安全关键控制、机器人和神经网络验证中至关重要，但经典方法（如Hamilton-Jacobi可达性和集合传播）随状态维度扩展性差。采样方法虽已成为有前景的替代方案，但初始集合的几何形状、动力学和采样分布如何影响估计精度的精确分析在文献中尚不完整。

Method: 将采样可达集恢复问题形式化为几何支撑估计，识别了两个关键正则性条件（初始集补集的正可达性和动力系统的Lipschitz连续性），在此基础上推导出概率质量覆盖到Hausdorff距离精度的提升以及样本复杂度上界，并通过极小化下界证明指数依赖的本质性。

Result: 样本复杂度上界为Õ((e^{3LT}/r)^n)，极小化下界为Ω((e^{LT}/r)^n)，表明对维度n和时域T的指数依赖是本质的；非线性系统实验验证了对抗采样只能改善常数而非缩放规律。

Conclusion: 采样法可达集恢复在维度和时间horizon上存在固有的指数复杂度瓶颈，两个正则性条件均是充分必要的，为未来设计更高效的算法提供了理论下限约束。

Abstract: Reachability analysis is central to safety-critical control, robotics, and neural network verification, but classical computational methods, such as Hamilton--Jacobi reachability and set propagation, scale poorly with state dimension. Sampling-based methods have emerged as a promising alternative, often providing finite-sample guarantees that bound the probability-mass left uncovered. However, an explicit account of how the geometry of the initial set, the dynamics, and the sampling law affect the accuracy of the estimator is not fully available in the literature. We study this by casting sampling-based reachable-set recovery as geometric support estimation over a family of problems specified by an initial set, its dynamics, and a sampling law. First, we identify two regularity properties, positive reach of the initial set's complement and Lipschitz continuity of the dynamics, that together make recovery well-posed: a probability-mass coverage guarantee can be upgraded to accuracy $r$ in Hausdorff distance. Second, we bound the resulting sample complexity: recovery is achievable with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big((e^{3LT}/r)^n\big)$ samples, exponential in both the state dimension and the time horizon. Third, we show that neither can be removed: an minimax lower bound of $Ω\big((e^{LT}/r)^n\big)$ holds for every estimator, so the exponential dependence on dimension and the degradation over the horizon are both intrinsic, not artifacts of a particular method. Experiments on nonlinear systems confirm that adversarial sampling improves constants but not the scaling.

</details>


### [25] [End-to-end Conditional Diffusion for Realistic and Controllable Visual Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18637)
*Jingzheng Li,Yufei Ge,Zhijun Chen,Qianren Mao,Zizhe Wang,Binhang Qi,Bing Li,Keyu Chen,Baochang Zhang,Xianglong Liu,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出E2E-CDiff，一种端到端条件扩散框架，用于生成可控且逼真的闭环交通场景。该方法以前视图视觉观测为条件，联合去噪未来运动状态和可执行的低层控制，并通过可微引导实现速度调节、行驶区域约束及碰撞行为控制，在Bench2Drive数据集上实现了可控性与真实性的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法难以平衡可控性与真实性，要么缺乏对交通行为的细粒度控制，要么在可控场景中牺牲了行为合理性。传统两阶段轨迹-控制器流水线存在规划与控制不匹配的问题。

Method: 基于条件扩散框架，以前视图视觉观测为条件，联合去噪生成未来运动状态和可执行低层控制信号（统一状态-动作生成），并引入可微引导机制来调节速度、约束可行驶区域、支持碰撞避免或碰撞诱导行为。

Result: 在Bench2Drive数据集上，E2E-CDiff相比代表性强化学习和模仿学习基线实现了更优的可控性-真实性权衡；其碰撞引导变体可在多个自动驾驶系统中诱发具有挑战性的交互；作为自车规划器也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 端到端状态-动作扩散方法有效解决了可控性与真实性的权衡问题，展现了在交通场景生成和自车规划中的通用性。

Abstract: Generating closed-loop traffic scenarios that are both realistic and controllable is crucial for evaluating autonomous driving systems, especially under rare safety-critical interactions. Existing learning-based methods often struggle to balance controllability and realism, offering either limited fine-grained control over traffic behavior or controllable scenarios at the expense of behavioral plausibility. This paper presents E2E-CDiff, an end-to-end conditional diffusion framework for controllable and realistic scenario generation. Conditioned on front-view visual observations, E2E-CDiff jointly denoises future motion states and executable low-level controls for route-interacting background vehicles. This unified state-action generation mitigates the planning-control mismatch in conventional two-stage trajectory-then-controller pipelines. Differentiable guidance further regulates speed, enforces drivable-area compliance, and supports collision-avoidance or collision-seeking behaviors, enabling both naturalistic and safety-critical scenario generation. Experiments on Bench2Drive show that E2E-CDiff achieves a favorable controllability-realism trade-off compared with representative reinforcement- and imitation-learning baselines, while its collision-guided variant induces challenging interactions across multiple autonomous driving systems. E2E-CDiff also performs competitively as a learning-based ego planner, demonstrating the generality of end-to-end state-action diffusion.

</details>


### [26] [Fabric Pneumatic Artificial Muscles Based on the Drawstring Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18641)
*Chendong Liu,Dapeng Yang,Yiming Dai,Li Jiang,Hong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于抽绳原理的织物气动人工肌肉（DPAM），在收缩时不会产生额外径向变形，具有高负载能力（超自重800倍）、最大收缩率44%、功率密度4.98 kW/kg等优异性能，并展示了在仿生机器人和工业生产线中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统气动人工肌肉在轴向收缩时会产生额外的径向变形，严重浪费可用工作空间，限制了其在紧凑结构中的应用。

Method: 受纺织品中广泛采用的抽绳原理启发，提出了一种新型抽绳织物气动人工肌肉（DPAM），通过织物结构设计实现收缩时不产生额外径向变形。

Result: DPAM具有超自重800倍的负载能力、44%的最大收缩率、4.98 kW/kg的功率密度，并具备优异的可扩展性；在二维平面内可轻松扩展为DPAM矩阵。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提出了一种高性能新型气动人工肌肉，还启发研究人员从传统纺织结构中汲取设计灵感，以应对软体机器人中的现有挑战。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have wide applications in robotics and industrial fields. Conventional pneumatic artificial muscles generate extra radial deformation during axial contraction, which severely wastes available working space. Inspired by the widely adopted drawstring principle in textile products, this paper proposes a novel drawstring fabric pneumatic artificial muscle (DPAM). Unlike traditional counterparts, the proposed DPAM produces no extra radial deformation during contraction, greatly improving structural compactness. The DPAM exhibits outstanding mechanical performance: a load capacity over 800 times its self-weight, a maximum contraction ratio of 44%, and a power density up to 4.98 kW/kg, alongside excellent scalability. Two representative application scenarios, bionic robots and industrial production lines, are demonstrated to validate its practicability. The DPAM can be easily expanded within a two-dimensional plane, as verified by the fabricated DPAM matrix. This work not only presents a high-performance novel pneumatic artificial muscle but also inspires researchers to draw design inspiration from conventional textile structures to address existing challenges in soft robotics.

</details>


### [27] [MVP-Tac: A Miniaturized Dual-Modal Vision and Photoelastic Tactile Sensor for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18660)
*Md Rakibul Islam Prince,Jaeeun Kim,Yuhao Zhou,Mason Vrshek,Shivani Reddy Sama,Adyaa Khera,Sheeraz Athar,Zijie Xu,Jiabin Liu,Shaoting Lin,Wei Li,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVP-Tac是一种基于反射光弹性成像的紧凑型视觉触觉传感器，通过半透明膜和可控照明实现视觉与触觉共位感知，在RMIS中达到97%（暴露肿瘤）和92%（皮下肿瘤）的硬度分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMIS缺乏触觉反馈，现有触觉方案难以与视觉兼容共存，亟需一种能同时提供视觉和触觉感知的实用化方案。

Method: 采用反射光弹性成像：薄光弹性弹性体在接触下产生应力相关干涉图，通过微型反射偏振镜由嵌入式相机捕获；半透明膜和可控照明实现视觉/触觉模式切换。

Result: 0-2N力校准；组织体模暴露肿瘤分类准确率97%，皮下肿瘤92%；模拟结肠镜中实现视觉引导3D光映射和原位硬度分类。

Conclusion: MVP-Tac为RMIS提供了一种在保持视觉反馈的同时恢复临床可用的触诊功能的实用路径，且已开源。

Abstract: Robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RMIS) offers major benefits over open and conventional laparoscopic procedures, yet it still lacks tactile feedback for palpation while operating under strict requirements to preserve reliable vision for navigation and safety. In practice, visual feedback is indispensable, and tactile solutions that cannot coexist with vision are difficult to translate into RMIS tools. To address both needs, we introduce MVP-Tac, a compact, vision-based tactile sensor that provides co-located vision and tactile sensing. MVP-Tac uses reflective photoelastic imaging: a thin photoelastic elastomer produces stress-dependent interferograms under contact that are captured by an embedded camera through a miniaturized reflective polariscope. A semi-transparent membrane and controllable illumination enable switching between visual mode and tactile mode, enabling tactile perception without sacrificing vision. We validate MVP-Tac through force calibration in the 0 to 2 N range and demonstrate its potential for tumor palpation via video-based hardness classification on tissue phantoms, achieving 97% accuracy for exposed-tumor classification and 92% accuracy for subdermal-tumor classification. Finally, we conduct a simulated colonoscopy to validate both visual and tactile modalities in a constrained lumen, including vision-guided 3D photomapping of the luminal wall and in situ hardness classification of localized nodules. Overall, MVP-Tac provides a practical path toward restoring clinically useful palpation in RMIS while maintaining essential visual feedback. The design, fabrication, and firmware of MVP-Tac are open-sourced at https://mvp-tac.github.io/

</details>


### [28] [How defensive driving enhances driving safety: A driving simulator study on drivers' defensive driving behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18663)
*Xinzheng Wu,Junyi Chen,Shaolingfeng Ye,Yong Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过驾驶模拟器实验，探究防御性驾驶的行为特征及其对驾驶安全的影响机制。结果显示，高防御性驾驶能力组表现出更剧烈的加速/减速、更低平均速度、更大横摆角，以及更短的注视时长和更低的注视频率，且更早采取避险行动，从而降低场景风险。低能力组在两次试验间表现出显著差异，而高能力组则无显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御性驾驶被广泛认为是高级驾驶技能，但关于其如何影响驾驶安全以及背后的作用机制仍研究不足，因此需要系统探究。

Method: 首先明确定义防御性驾驶的操作时机与应用场景；随后招募82名参与者进行驾驶模拟器实验，采集行为和眼动数据；根据防御性驾驶行为频率分组；进行组间和试验间比较分析。

Result: 高防御性驾驶能力组加速/减速幅度更大、平均速度更低、平均绝对横摆角更大，注视时长更短、注视频率更低；该组更早启动避险动作，场景风险更低。低能力组在两次试验间表现出显著差异，而高能力组则无显著变化。

Conclusion: 具备防御性驾驶能力的驾驶员倾向于执行更激烈的驾驶操作，并更早识别风险和采取行动，从而提升驾驶安全性。研究成果支持推广防御性驾驶，为相关培训项目提供依据，并为具备防御性驾驶能力的自动驾驶算法训练提供参考。

Abstract: Defensive driving is widely recognized as an advanced driving skill. However, whether and how defensive driving affects driving safety remains insufficiently investigated. This study examines the behavioral characteristics of defensive driving, its impact on driving safety, and the underlying mechanisms. First, defensive driving is defined regarding operational timing and application scenario. Then, 82 participants are recruited for driving simulator experiments, with their behavioral and eye movement data being collected. Following the experiments, participants are categorized into groups based on the frequency of defensive driving behaviors exhibited. Finally, both inter-group and inter trial comparisons are performed on the experimental data. Experimental results demonstrate that in the inter-group comparison, the high defensive driving capability group exhibits higher acceleration and deceleration magnitudes, lower average speeds, and larger average absolute yaw angles compared to the low capability group, alongside shorter fixation durations and reduced fixation frequencies. Moreover, we observe that these participants tend to initiate defensive or evasive actions earlier, resulting in lower scenario risk. Regarding the inter-trial comparison, we observe similar trends exclusively in the low capability group, whereas most metrics show no significant differences between Trial 1 and Trial 2 in the high capability group. These results reveal that drivers possessing defensive driving capabilities tend to execute more intense driving maneuvers and identify risks and take action earlier, thereby enhancing driving safety. Findings support the promotion of defensive driving and provide a basis for relevant training programs. Meanwhile, they offer insights for the training of autonomous driving algorithms with defensive driving capabilities.

</details>


### [29] [RoboInter1.5: A Holistic Intermediate Representation Suite for Embodied World Modeling and Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18709)
*Ziqin Wang,Hao Li,Weijun Wang,Junhao Cai,Jia Zeng,Yilun Chen,Jiangmiao Pang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboInter1.5 是一个面向机器人操作和具身世界建模的扩展统一套件，提供超23万操作片段、十多种细粒度中间表示（子任务、技能、抓取位姿、接触点等）的稠密逐帧标注，以及基于这些标注构建的VQA推理、VLA执行和World未来状态预测三大基准与模型，将中间表示定位为双向接口——既正则化底层动作空间，又约束开放世界物理模拟器的潜在推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集存在成本高昂、具身专用性强、缺乏支持通用推理/执行/长程动力学仿真的细粒度结构标注三大问题。

Method: 在RoboInter1.0基础上扩展，构建包含571场景、超23万操作片段的稠密逐帧标注数据集（RoboInter-Data），覆盖子任务、技能、物体/夹爪定位、分割、可供性、抓取位姿、接触点、运动轨迹等十余种中间表示。基于此构建三类任务：RoboInter-VQA（时空具身VQA推理）、RoboInter-VLA（隐式/显式/模块化规划-执行范式）、RoboInter-World（以中间表示为结构化条件信号控制未来世界状态预测）。

Result: 大量评估表明RoboInter1.5为中间表示提供了统一的时空骨架，中间表示不仅能作为可解释信号，还能作为双向接口：正则化底层动作空间并约束开放世界物理模拟器的潜在推演。

Conclusion: RoboInter1.5将中间表示概念从可解释信号提升为双向接口，统一了机器人操作与具身世界建模的数据、基准和模型体系，为通用推理、执行和物理仿真提供了结构化的中间层支撑。

Abstract: Existing robot datasets remain expensive to curate, embodiment-specific, and insufficiently annotated with the fine-grained structure required for generalizable reasoning, execution, or long-horizon environment dynamics simulation. Building on our prior work, RoboInter1.0, we present RoboInter1.5, an extended and holistic suite of intermediate representations for both robotic manipulation and embodied world modeling. RoboInter1.5 provides a unified resource of data, benchmarks, and models centered on dense manipulation-oriented intermediate representations. Specifically, RoboInter-Data contains over 230k manipulation episodes across 571 scenes with dense per-frame annotations covering more than ten types of intermediate representations, including subtasks, primitive skills, object and gripper grounding, segmentation, affordance, grasp poses, contact points, motion traces, etc. Built upon these annotations, RoboInter-VQA introduces spatial and temporal embodied VQA tasks to benchmark and improve the intermediate-representation reasoning capabilities of our RoboInter-VLM. RoboInter-VLA further studies how such representations benefit action execution through implicit, explicit, and modular plan-then-execute paradigms. To better model the physical world, we further introduce RoboInter-World, which leverages intermediate representations as structured conditioning signals for controllable prediction of future world states. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that RoboInter1.5 provides a unified spatiotemporal scaffolding for intermediate representations. Rather than treating intermediate representations merely as interpretable signals, RoboInter1.5 conceptualizes them as a bidirectional interface that both regularizes low-level action spaces and constrains the latent rollouts of open-world physical simulators.

</details>


### [30] [Confidence-Gated Vision-Only Heading Alignment for UAV-UGV Cooperative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18713)
*Reza Ahmari,Vahid Hemmati,Parham Kebria,Olusola Odeyomi,Kaushik Roy,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对视域引导的UAV-UGV协同中，仅靠航向预测精度不足以直接发布控制指令的问题，提出了一种轻量级置信门控框架。该框架利用感知流中两个可解释的可靠性代理——边界框面积（可见性代理）和预测航向短窗变化（稳定性代理）来决定何时以及如何信任固定航向预测器。在低置信度区间，比较了基线冻结保持策略与保守更新的有界混合回退策略。在真实UAV-UGV数据集上的实验表明，置信门控在执行率、执行帧准确率、发布指令准确率和平滑性之间存在显著权衡，并且稀疏执行在冻结保持策略下会产生严重的过时指令误差，而有界混合回退策略在相同门控决策下显著改善了指令级行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视域的航向预测虽然精度高，但仅依靠预测精度无法保证每个预测结果都适合直接作为控制指令发布。需要研究何时以及如何信任预测器以做出指令发布决策。

Method: 提出轻量级置信门控框架，使用两种可解释的可靠性代理：边界框面积（可见性代理）和预测航向短窗变化（稳定性代理）。在低置信度区间比较基线冻结保持策略与有界混合回退策略。

Result: 置信门控在执行率、执行帧准确率、发布指令准确率和平滑性之间存在权衡；稀疏执行在冻结保持策略下产生严重过时指令误差；有界混合回退策略在相同门控决策下显著改善指令级行为。

Conclusion: 可靠的感知驱动自主性不仅依赖预测精度，还需要在低置信度期间进行决策感知的指令发布。

Abstract: Vision-based heading prediction is useful for UAV--UGV cooperation, but accurate prediction alone does not guarantee that every predicted heading should be issued directly as a control command. This paper investigates the decision problem of when and how a fixed vision-based heading predictor should be trusted for command issuance. A lightweight confidence-gated framework is proposed in which execution decisions are made using two interpretable reliability proxies derived from the perception stream: bounding-box area as a visibility-related proxy and short-window variation in predicted heading as a stability-related proxy. During low-confidence intervals, the framework compares the baseline freeze-HOLD policy with a bounded-blend fallback that updates the issued command conservatively. The method is evaluated on a real UAV--UGV dataset under clean and perturbed conditions. The results show that confidence gating creates a clear trade-off among execution rate, executed-frame accuracy, issued-command accuracy, and smoothness. The results further show that sparse execution can cause severe stale-command error under the baseline freeze-HOLD policy, whereas the bounded-blend fallback substantially improves command-level behavior under the same gate decisions. These findings highlight that reliable perception-driven autonomy depends not only on prediction accuracy, but also on decision-aware command issuance during low-confidence

</details>


### [31] [Correct-by-Construction Behavior Tree Synthesis from Signal Temporal Logic Specifications with Application to Robotic Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18731)
*Jiaheng Dong,Jingyi Huang,Liang Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从信号时序逻辑（STL）规范正确合成行为树（BT）的方法，通过将工作空间建模为时间迁移系统并抽象为区域图，引入追踪逻辑进展和时序约束的增广状态空间，利用分层不动点算法计算获胜集，生成带有运行时约束函数的BT子树，并在仿真和实物四旋翼实验中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为树合成方法缺乏形式化保证，且基于线性时序逻辑（LTL）的合成无法表达定量时序约束，限制了其在需要严格时间要求的机器人任务中的应用。

Method: 将工作空间建模为时间迁移系统并抽象为区域图，引入增广状态空间同时追踪逻辑进展和时序约束，设计分层不动点算法为STL片段（安全、到达、响应、往复、持久）计算获胜集，生成BT子树并附带运行时约束函数。

Result: 证明正确性并推导复杂度上界，仿真验证了规范满足且具有严格正鲁棒性，实物四旋翼实验在6个STL规范下验证了实际可部署性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将STL规范正确合成到BT中，弥补了LTL方法无法处理定量时序约束的不足，兼顾了BT的模块化反应控制与形式化保证，具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Behavior Trees (BTs) are widely adopted for complex task execution in robotics, providing modular, reactive control but lacking formal guarantees. However, existing correct-by-construction synthesis from Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) cannot express quantitative timing constraints. This letter synthesizes correct-by-construction BTs from Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications. The workspace is modeled as a timed transition system and abstracted into a zone graph, and an augmented state space tracking both logical progress and timing constraints is introduced. A hierarchical fixed-point algorithm computes winning sets for an STL fragment encompassing safety, reachability, response, recurrence, and persistence, yielding BT subtrees with a runtime constraint function. Correctness guarantees are proven and complexity bounds are derived. Simulations demonstrate specification satisfaction with strictly positive robustness, and a physical quadrotor experiment with six STL specifications validates practical deployability.

</details>


### [32] [Motion Primitive Discovery in a Humanoid Robot via Self-Organising Maps for Phase Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18737)
*Radovan Gregor,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受镜像神经元系统启发的双层架构，用于机器人运动基元发现和在线相位识别。第一层使用两种自组织映射网络（A-SOM和H-SOM）学习手臂和手部运动学的地形表征；第二层使用回声状态网络对SOM激活轨迹进行在线运动相位识别。实验表明该架构能准确识别当前执行的运动阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作识别是社会认知和人机交互的核心挑战，受镜像神经元系统启发，研究者希望构建一种能够实现运动基元发现和在线相位识别的计算架构，以赋予机器人类似生物体的动作理解能力。

Method: 提出双层架构：第一层利用两个自组织映射网络（A-SOM和H-SOM），通过层次相关分析提取非冗余特征，对七种模拟运动动作中的手臂和手部运动学数据进行地形表征学习；第二层使用回声状态网络，将连续的SOM最佳匹配单元轨迹作为输入，评估其对在线识别当前执行运动相位的效果。

Result: 两个SOM编码了运动行为互补的方面；基于SOM的轨迹保留了运动的主导相位判别结构，上下文信息仅提供次要的改进作用；该架构能够支持准确的在线运动相位识别。

Conclusion: 将自组织映射和回声状态网络集成到受MNS启发的架构中，是有效的运动基元表征和在线相位识别方法。结果支持了计算假设：自组织运动表征在时间整合后能够支持对当前运动阶段进行准确的在线识别。

Abstract: Understanding the computational basis of action recognition is a central challenge in social cognition as well as in human-robot interaction. Inspired by the Mirror Neuron System (MNS), we propose a two-level architecture for motor primitive discovery and online phase recognition applied to the NICO humanoid robot. At the first level, two Self-Organising Maps (SOMs) learn topographic representations of arm kinematics (A-SOM) and hand kinematics (H-SOM) from simulated trials covering seven motor actions. The maps are trained on non-redundant features identified through hierarchical correlation analysis of motion trajectories. The results show that the two SOMs encode complementary aspects of motor behaviour. At the second level, an Echo State Network (ESN) evaluates whether temporal trajectories of SOM activations, represented by consecutive best-matching units, are sufficient for online recognition of the currently executed movement phase. The results show that SOM-based trajectories preserve the dominant phase-discriminative structure of the movement, while contextual information provides only a secondary refinement. Our contribution is the integration of established SOM and ESN methods within an MNS-inspired architecture for motor primitive representation and online phase recognition. The results are compatible with the computational hypothesis that self-organised motor representations, when temporally integrated, can support accurate online recognition of ongoing movement phases.

</details>


### [33] [Koopman DCM: Unstable Eigenfunctions as Data-driven Representations for Legged Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18760)
*Stéphane Caron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将散度分量（DCM）一般化为Koopman特征函数，通过数据驱动方式从真实机器人数据中学习不稳定特征对，并将其应用于双足机器人的平衡控制和行走模式跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DCM公式仅适用于简化模型（如线性倒立摆），缺乏通用性。作者希望将DCM扩展为更一般的形式，使其能够直接从真实机器人数据中学习，而不依赖于简化模型假设。

Method: 利用Koopman算子理论，将DCM重新定义为Koopman特征函数。与传统Koopman分析关注零附近特征值不同，本文故意搜索具有大特征值的不稳定特征对。使用一小时的真实双足机器人数据，通过数据驱动方式学习DCM。

Result: 在真实双足机器人上，学习到的DCM改善了对参考行走模式的跟踪。结合模型预测控制时，学习到的DCM能够提供基于状态的可行性约束。

Conclusion: Koopman DCM是一种通用的、数据驱动的平衡状态表示方法，能够从真实机器人数据中学习，并有效提升双足机器人的行走控制性能。

Abstract: In legged locomotion, divergent components of motion (DCMs) have emerged as characteristic states for balance control. They isolate the unstable mode of the dynamics but, in existing formulations, apply only to reduced models such as the linear inverted pendulum. In this study, we show how DCMs can be more generally formulated as Koopman eigenfunctions. Whereas Koopman analysis typically targets eigenvalues near zero, which capture conserved or slowly varying quantities, our investigation leads us to deliberately search for unstable eigenpairs with large eigenvalues. The resulting Koopman DCMs are data-driven observables trained using only real-robot data. On a real biped, DCMs learned from one hour of robot data improve tracking of reference walking patterns. We further show how learned DCMs provide state-based viability constraints when combined with model predictive control.

</details>


### [34] [Beyond Transformers: Linear Attention Policy for Open-Vocabulary Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18794)
*Jiahong Zhang,Yifan Lin,Yandong Zhang,Sijun Shen,Kexin Wang,Yuqi Pan,Hongjuan Pei,Wei Wang,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于线性注意力（Linear Attention）的导航策略LANav，用于开放词汇目标导航（OVON），通过结构化状态更新替代传统Transformer中的自注意力机制，解决了上下文长度扩展时性能不提升的问题。其中WSLA变体通过多头状态扩展与可学习加权读出进一步提升了效果，在HM3D-OVON上平均成功率36.4%，比Transformer基线高6.3个百分点，实物部署成功率达82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer策略在部分可观测的OVON任务中，通过自注意力在上下文窗口上整合时间信息，但实验表明性能并不随上下文长度扩展而提升，质疑了自注意力作为导航状态更新机制的有效性。因此需要一种更结构化的状态更新方式。

Method: 提出LANav，采用线性注意力（LA）替代自注意力作为策略骨干网络，维护结构化的状态更新；进一步提出WSLA（Weighted State-Expansion Linear Attention），将每个注意力头扩展为多个子状态，并通过可学习加权读出聚合这些子状态，增强状态更新的表达能力。

Result: 在HM3D-OVON上平均SR达36.4%，比Transformer基线高6.3个百分点；远距离场景增益更大；HSSD迁移和微调验证了场景分布鲁棒性；Unitree Go2实物部署50次试验成功率达82%。

Conclusion: 线性注意力作为策略骨干在OVON中优于Transformer自注意力，结构化状态更新机制对导航性能至关重要；WSLA通过子状态扩展与可学习读出进一步提升了状态更新效果；LANav兼具计算效率、场景鲁棒性与Sim-to-Real可行性。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Object Goal Navigation (OVON) requires agents to operate under partial observability, making effective internal state updates critical for navigation performance. This update is implemented by the policy network, where recent approaches adopt Transformer-based backbones with self-attention over a context window to integrate temporal information. However, our controlled experiments show that performance does not scale with context length under Transformer-based policies, questioning the suitability of self-attention for state integration in navigation. To this end, we propose Linear Attention-based Navigation (LANav), which adopts linear attention (LA) as the policy backbone to maintain a structured state update rather than self-attention over the context window. Across multiple LA variants evaluated under identical settings, LANav consistently outperforms Transformer-based baselines. Performance improves as state update mechanisms become more structured and regulated, highlighting the importance of state update design. To improve state update effectiveness, we introduce Weighted State-Expansion Linear Attention (WSLA), which expands each attention head's state into multiple sub-states and uses learnable weighted readout to aggregate expanded sub-states. Equipped with WSLA, LANav achieves 36.4% average success rate (SR) on HM3D-OVON, outperforming Transformer-based counterparts by 6.3 percentage points in macro-averaged SR, while maintaining computational efficiency. Distance-stratified results show larger gains in long-distance episodes, while HSSD transfer and fine-tuning demonstrate robustness across scene distributions. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 further achieves an 82% success rate over 50 trials, supporting the practical feasibility and sim-to-real transfer of LANav.

</details>

*Haisheng Su,Zongdai Liu,Xin Jin,Haoxuan Dou,Chengming Hu,Baorun Li,Zhanwang Liu,Ruiyan Xu,Jianjie Fang,Xin Zhang,Zhenjie Yang,Xue Yang,Chen Gao,Junchi Yan,Yong Li,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 WorldScape Policy 2.0，一种具有推理增强型长短期记忆的可控世界动作模型，通过短期视觉记忆、长期事件记忆和显式事件级数据集 ManipEvent-5M，实现了长程自主规划、细粒度指令跟随和跨具身上下文适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）受限于有限的时间上下文、粗粒度的 episode 级语言监督以及以文本为主的条件输入，导致难以跟踪任务进度、无法实现细粒度的语言-视频-动作对齐，且缺乏视觉上下文推理与跨具身迁移能力。

Method: 提出 WorldScape Policy 2.0 框架：(1) 因果短期视觉记忆将最近观察作为 DiT 预填充以保留局部交互动态；(2) 长短期事件记忆将历史VLM输出组织为全局历史、局部活跃和事件边界三种表示，实现进度感知的检索；(3) 检索的历史增强感知和自回归生成的规划token，产生隐式子目标条件用于自主规划；(4) 语义强制将事件级指令语义注入潜在规划路径；(5) 构建 ManipEvent-5M 数据集，包含近500万个事件片段及其对齐的动作轨迹、任务指令、子任务描述、目标图像和视频演示。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验中，该方法在长程自主规划、细粒度指令跟随和上下文自适应方面表现出优越能力。

Conclusion: 通过推理增强的长短期记忆和事件级多模态数据集，该方法为从高层指令的自主规划到细粒度文本/目标图像/视频上下文提示的可控执行提供了统一接口，有效解决了现有WAMs的多项局限性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by jointly modeling visual state transitions and robot actions. However, existing WAMs are constrained by limited temporal context, coarse episode-level language supervision, and predominantly text-only conditioning, which hinder task-progress tracking and fine-grained language-video-action grounding while limiting visual-context reasoning and cross-embodiment transfer. In this paper, we introduce WorldScape Policy 2.0, a controllable WAM with reasoning-augmented long short-term memory. Its causal short-term visual memory supplies recent observations as DiT prefill to preserve local interaction dynamics, while its long short-term event memory organizes historical VLM outputs into global-history, local-active, and event-boundary representations for progress-aware retrieval. The retrieved history augments perception and autoregressively generated planning tokens, yielding an implicit subgoal condition for autonomous planning; semantic forcing further transfers event-level instruction semantics into this latent planning pathway. To establish fine-grained multimodal controllability, we construct ManipEvent-5M, an event-grounded embodied pretraining dataset containing nearly 5 million event segments with aligned action trajectories, episode-level task instructions, segment-level subtask captions, goal images, and video demonstrations. These designs provide a unified interface for autonomous planning from high-level instructions and controllable execution from fine-grained text, goal-image, or video-context prompts. Experiments in both simulation and real-world platforms demonstrate superior capabilities in long-horizon autonomous planning, fine-grained instruction following and in-context adaptation.

</details>

*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Tuomo Kivelä,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对长臂钻机（Sandvik SB60）的自避障运动规划问题，将姿态参数化规划与基于胶囊碰撞模型的控制障碍函数二次规划（CBF-QP）相结合，在测量状态逆运动学框架中实现了无自碰撞的自主运动。离线绕路规划器通过23个杆节-身体区域距离筛选候选路径点，在线CBF-QP通过14个胶囊对约束滤波关节速度。在两个钻孔任务（五目标受限姿态巡游和三目标全姿态巡游）的Simscape仿真中，该方法零IK失败、产生多个绕路路径点、达到毫米级末端定位精度，且CBF安全裕度均高于设定阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长臂钻机在连续作业中需到达多个位姿且避免自碰撞，当前从人工监督向自主控制过渡缺乏有效的碰撞感知运动规划与执行方法。

Method: 将姿态参数化规划与基于胶囊模型的控制障碍函数二次规划（CBF-QP）集成到测量状态逆运动学（IK）中；离线阶段用23个杆段-身体区域距离筛选候选路径点；在线阶段用14个胶囊对约束通过CBF-QP滤波关节速度；采用九基元全身胶囊模型进行碰撞建模。

Result: 在Sandvik SB60 Simscape Multibody仿真模型中，五目标受限姿态巡游和三目标全姿态巡游任务中，数十万样本内零IK失败、产生多个绕路路径点、毫米级平均末端位置误差、CBF裕度均高于报告阈值。

Conclusion: 所提出的离线绕路规划与在线CBF-QP结合的方法能有效实现长臂钻机无自碰撞的自主运动规划与执行，无需针对不同目标重新调整参数。

Abstract: Long-reach drilling booms must reach successive poses without self-collision. Moving from operator-supervised control toward autonomy requires collision-aware motion planning and execution. For the Sandvik SB60, this study adapts established methods by integrating pose-parameterized planning with a capsule-based control barrier function quadratic program (CBF-QP) in measured-state inverse kinematics (IK). A fixed task-specific parameter set within each task generates waypoints, detours, timed references, and chained motion without target-specific retuning. The offline detour planner screens candidate waypoints using 23 selected rod-segment-to-body-region distances, whereas the online CBF-QP filters joint velocities using 14 configured capsule-pair constraints from a nine-primitive whole-body capsule model. Evaluation considers two drilling tasks in a manufacturer-developed SB60 Simscape Multibody model: a five-target restricted-orientation tour and a three-target full-pose tour. Across several hundred thousand samples, the method produced zero IK failures, generated several detour waypoints, achieved millimetre-level mean final-position error, and recorded no sampled CBF margins below the reported thresholds.

</details>


### [37] [NGPS: GPS-Denied Aerial Geo-Localization and 2.5D Reconstruction via Deep Satellite Image Matching and Multi-Rate Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18936)
*Sanket Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NGPS是一个面向高空无人机、基于深度学习特征的视觉地理定位框架，通过将下视图像与地理参考卫星图像匹配实现无GPS绝对定位。系统融合了自适应置信加权UKF融合、速度预测核提取和非同步多速率时序优先级队列，在60-150米AGL的五次飞行序列上实现了2.94米的位置RMSE，相对单目VIO提升3.5倍，并在Jetson Orin NX上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高空无人机在GPS信号不可用或受干扰场景下的绝对定位问题，克服传统VIO在大尺度高空环境下漂移严重、无绝对位置基准的局限性。

Method: 提出NGPS框架，核心包括：(1) 自适应置信加权UKF融合，利用RANSAC内点率、重投影误差和匹配置信度动态调制NGPS协方差；(2) 速度预测核提取，利用VIO速度预测卫星图像搜索区域；(3) 非同步多速率时序优先级队列，按时间顺序交错融合绝对定位(1-2Hz)、VIO(10-20Hz)和IMU(100-200Hz)更新；并通过VINS位姿图优化锚定NGPS校正，实现实时2.5D地理参考正射拼接重建。

Result: 在五种飞行序列（60-150米AGL）上，NGPS达到2.94米位置RMSE，最差ATE为150米AGL和2m/s条件下的6.04米，相较于纯单目VIO实现了3.5倍的精度提升，系统在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上实时运行。

Conclusion: NGPS成功实现了高空无人机在无GPS条件下的高精度绝对视觉定位，有效解决了高空VIO漂移问题，具有实时性和部分开源的优势，适用于GPS拒止环境下的无人机自主导航和测绘任务。

Abstract: We present NGPS (Next-Generation Positioning System), a visual geo-localization framework for high-altitude UAVs that provides GPS-free absolute positioning by matching down-facing images to georeferenced satellite imagery with deep features. The system combines (1) adaptive confidence-weighted UKF fusion, where NGPS covariance is modulated by RANSAC inlier ratio, reprojection error, and match confidence; (2) velocity-predictive kernel extraction, using VIO velocity to predict the satellite search region; and (3) an asynchronous multi-rate temporal priority queue that interleaves absolute position (1-2 Hz), VIO (10-20 Hz), and IMU (100-200 Hz) updates in chronological order. Globally optimized poses from VINS pose-graph optimization, anchored by NGPS corrections, further enable real-time 2.5D georeferenced orthomosaic reconstruction. On five flight sequences (60-150 m AGL), NGPS achieves 2.94 m position RMSE, with worst-case ATE 6.04 m at 150 m AGL and 2 m/s, yielding a 3.5x improvement over standalone monocular VIO. The system runs in real time on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. Part of the implementation is open-sourced at https://github.com/snktshrma/ngps_flight.

</details>
