### [89] [Exotic Electronic Order in a Parabolic Kagome Semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18389)
*C. Alexander Baum,Jonas Issing,Sarbajit Mazumdar,Matteo Dürrnagel,Michael Klett,Ronny Thomale,Lennart Klebl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过功能重整化群和奴隶玻色子方法，研究了Kagome晶格上2/3填充的二次能带接触半金属在中等至强相互作用下的相图，发现了包括环电流序、自发交变磁性和自旋环电流序在内的非常规电子序层级结构，并揭示了能带运动学、电子相互作用和量子几何如何共同决定这些相的选取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二次能带接触半金属在强关联下的非常规对称性破缺现象，揭示能带运动学、电子相互作用与量子几何对非常规电子序的调控机制。

Method: 结合功能重整化群（fRG）和奴隶玻色子方法，从中等耦合到强耦合区域绘制相图。

Result: 发现了环电流序、自旋Pomeranchuk不稳定性导致的自发交变磁性，以及具有独特且尚未充分探索性质的自旋环电流序等非常规电子序的层级结构。

Conclusion: 二次能带接触半金属是实现非常规对称性破缺的有前景平台，并暗示在其他抛物线型半金属中存在类似现象。

Abstract: We study an interacting kagome-lattice realization of a quadratic band-touching semimetal at 2/3 filling with onsite and nearest-neighbor repulsive interactions. Combining functional renormalization group and slave-boson approaches, we map its phase diagram from intermediate to strong coupling and uncover a hierarchy of unconventional electronic orders. The leading instabilities comprise loop-current order, spontaneous altermagnetism arising from a spin-Pomeranchuk instability, and spin-loop-current order with distinctive and largely unexplored properties. We demonstrate how the interplay of band kinematics, electronic interactions, and quantum geometry governs the selection of these phases. Our findings establish quadratic band-touching semimetals as a promising platform for unconventional symmetry breaking and suggest analogous phenomena in other parabolic semimetals.

</details>


### [90] [Non-Fermi-Liquid Behaviors in Weakly Interacting Two-Dimensional Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18394)
*Junmo Jeon,Shiro Sakai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过弱耦合微扰框架数值研究了Penrose和Ammann-Beenker准晶中的非费米液体行为，发现准晶因缺乏平移对称性导致的奇异态密度能量依赖，可在弱相互作用下产生反常热力学、磁性和输运性质，包括准粒子破坏和剩余散射率增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索准晶在弱相互作用下产生非费米液体行为的微观机制，揭示其与强关联体系（如量子临界性、奇异涨落）不同的物理根源——即准周期结构本身的单粒子态临界性和谱碎裂效应。

Method: 采用Penrose和Ammann-Beenker两类代表性准周期拼图，在弱耦合微扰框架下数值计算热力学（Sommerfeld系数）、磁性（磁化率、Wilson比）和输运（自能、剩余散射率）性质，并结合局域态密度进行位点分辨分析。

Result: Sommerfeld系数和磁化率呈现由费米能附近态密度的奇异能量依赖所决定的反常温度依赖；Wilson比在宽填充范围内显示增强的自旋涨落，但其大小和温度依赖敏感于填充率；自能呈现反常标度行为且剩余散射率有限，表明准粒子破坏；局域态密度高的区域剩余散射率显著增强。

Conclusion: 准晶中的非费米液体行为源于准周期结构本身的奇异电子态，无需强关联或量子临界性；结果为解释准晶实验数据并识别非费米液体行为的微观机制提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Non-Fermi-liquid (NFL) behavior is usually associated with strong electronic correlations, quantum criticality, or singular fluctuations that invalidate the quasiparticle picture. Quasicrystals are known to provide a fundamentally different route to such physics: their lack of translational symmetry gives rise to critical single-particle states and fragmented spectra even in the absence of electron-electron interactions. Here, we numerically study the NFL behaviors in quasicrystals with weak interactions. Using Penrose and Ammann--Beenker tilings as representative quasiperiodic systems, we analyze thermodynamic, magnetic, and transport properties within a weak-coupling perturbative framework. The Sommerfeld coefficient and magnetic susceptibility show anomalous temperature dependences governed by the singular energy dependence of the density of states near the Fermi level. The Wilson ratio reveals that low-temperature NFL behavior in quasicrystals is accompanied by enhanced spin fluctuations over a broad range of filling fractions, while its magnitude and temperature dependence vary sensitively with filling fraction. The self-energy shows anomalous scaling and a finite residual scattering rate, indicating the breakdown of quasiparticles. Our site-resolved analysis reveals a pronounced enhancement of the residual scattering rate in regions with a high local density of states. These results serve as a guide to analyze experimental data for quasicrystals and to identify the microscopic mechanism of the NFL behaviors.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum critical fan and emergent relativistic symmetry of two-dimensional Dirac semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18396)
*Friederike Ihssen,Bilal Hawashin,Mireia Tolosa-Simeón,Michael M. Scherer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用非微扰场论方法，系统研究了手征Ising模型的Gross–Neveu–Yukawa相图，包括零温量子临界点和有限温经典相变，揭示了量子临界扇区的标度性质与准粒子权重行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来高度可调的范德瓦尔斯异质结构中观测到狄拉克半金属到绝缘体的转变，迫切需要理解其中的有限温度效应，因此需要建立手征Ising模型在量子临界点附近的完整相图理论框架。

Method: 采用非微扰场论方法（非微扰重正化群等）研究Gross–Neveu–Yukawa模型，分别在零温（半金属相和自发破缺Z₂对称性的绝缘相）和有限温度下进行分析。

Result: 零温下发现量子临界点，临界指数接近手征Ising普适类，且相对论对称性在临界点附近涌现；有限温下有序相在临界温度以上转变为无序相，该相变属于二维Ising普适类；确定了量子临界扇区的范围和标度性质，以及其中准粒子权重的行为。

Conclusion: 为手征Ising模型在其量子临界点附近提供了统一的场论框架，系统描述了从量子到经典临界行为的完整相图。

Abstract: Two-dimensional Dirac semimetals near a quantum critical point can be described by Gross--Neveu--Yukawa models. In view of recent experimental advances exhibiting a transition from Dirac semimetal to insulator in highly-tunable van-der-Waals heterostructures, a better understanding of finite-temperature effects is mandatory. Here, we study the Gross--Neveu--Yukawa phase diagram of the chiral Ising model with a non-perturbative field-theory approach at zero and finite temperature, both in the semimetallic phase and in the insulating phase with spontaneously broken $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. At zero temperature, we find a quantum critical point with critical exponents that are close to the ones of the chiral Ising universality class, and show that relativistic symmetry is emergent close to the quantum critical point. At finite temperature, the ordered phase survives up to a finite critical temperature, at which we observe a classical phase transition into the disordered phase. We confirm that this transition lies in the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Finally, we determine the extent and scaling properties of the quantum critical fan, and the behavior of the quasiparticle weight, therein. In summary, we present a unified field-theoretical framework for the phase diagram of the chiral Ising model in the surroundings of its quantum critical point.

</details>


### [92] [Coherent Magnons Driven by Photomodulated Anisotropy in Altermagnetic MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18421)
*Dingbin Huang,Jonathon Kruppe,Resham Babu Regmi,Nirmal J. Ghimire,James Analytis,Joseph Orenstein*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过空间映射技术，在反铁磁材料MnTe中同时测量了Néel矢量的平衡取向、光激发自旋波的振幅和频率，发现其自旋波能隙极低（≤60 μeV），六重各向异性极弱，使得光致增强各向异性可驱动相干自旋波，并实现对电子能带结构中自旋劈裂取向的光学和机械调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnTe作为g波交替磁体的代表性材料，为研究交替磁序的非平衡激发提供了理想平台。作者旨在揭示其自旋波动力学特性及光学可调性。

Method: 通过同时空间映射局域平衡Néel矢量取向φ_L(r)、光激发自旋波振幅Δφ(r,t)和频率Ω(r)，测量自旋波能隙上限，分析六重各向异性常数K_6及其光致增强效应。

Result: 自旋波能隙上限约为60 μeV（0.7 K），六重各向异性极弱；高于阈值泵浦通量下，光致调制表现为Δφ对φ_L的六重对称锯齿依赖关系及Ω的摆线状调制。

Conclusion: MnTe中Néel矢量的近各向同性使得可通过光学和机械手段控制电子能带结构中的自旋劈裂取向，为交替磁体自旋电子学提供新途径。

Abstract: Manganese telluride ($\text{MnTe}$) has recently emerged as a prototypical $g$-wave altermagnet, providing an ideal platform to investigate the non-equilibrium excitations of altermagnetic order. Here, we report simultaneous spatial mapping of the local equilibrium orientation of the Néel vector, $\varphi_L(\mathbf{r})$, alongside the amplitude, $Δ\varphi(\mathbf{r},t)$, and frequency, $Ω(\mathbf{r})$, of photoexcited spin waves. Based on these measurements, we place a remarkably low upper bound of $\approx 60~μ\text{eV}$ (0.7 K) on the spin-wave gap arising from intrinsic anisotropy. This exceptionally weak hexagonal anisotropy ($K_6$) renders the altermagnetic order highly susceptible to optical tuning, allowing coherent spin waves to be driven by a photoinduced enhancement of $K_6$. Above a threshold pump fluence, our spatial maps reveal that this photomodulation manifests as a six-fold symmetric sawtooth dependence of $Δ\varphi$ on $\varphi_L$ and a cycloid-like modulation of $Ω$. Ultimately, the near-isotropy of the Néel vector in $\text{MnTe}$ enables optical and mechanical control over the orientation of spin-splitting in the electronic band structure, offering new pathways for altermagnetic spintronics.

</details>


### [93] [Theory of two-dimensional Wigner crystals with defects: Interactions, melting transitions and collective modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18477)
*Paweł Matus,Tobias Holder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用对偶技术构建了一个二维带电晶体缺陷（空位、间隙原子、位错、向错）的有效场论框架，系统研究了缺陷对的相互作用能、缺陷增殖导致的熔化相变，以及金属性Wigner晶体中空位费米液体的奇特声子色散关系与热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上观察到二维Wigner晶体的晶格熔化及相邻相，但缺陷如何影响这类固体的长波性质尚不明确，亟需建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 采用对偶技术（duality techniques）构建有效作用量，系统分析空位、间隙原子、位错和向错等缺陷对二维带电晶体长波性质的贡献。

Result: 获得了不同缺陷对组合的相互作用能；在金属性Wigner晶体相（基态存在有限空位密度）中发现声子色散关系以异常方式随空位密度变化；厘清了文献中关于熔化相变的部分假设。

Conclusion: 该场论框架可作为研究反常霍尔晶体和含缺陷电荷密度波的起点，且空位费米液体的热力学性质可通过熔融温度和声速测量来探测。

Abstract: The physics of Wigner solids is characterized by an interplay of elasticity and long-range electrostatics, endowing crystal defects with properties distinct from those in charge-neutral crystals. Recent experiments observing lattice melting and Wigner-crystal-adjacent phases in two dimensions necessitate an examination of how defects affect the long-wavelength properties of such solids. Here, we use duality techniques to construct a comprehensive framework for studying the contribution of vacancies and interstitials, dislocations, and disclinations to the effective action of two-dimensional charged crystals. This allows for a systematic investigation of the interaction energies for the different combinations of defect pairs. We further study melting transitions due to defect proliferation, assessing and justifying some of the assumptions present in the literature. In the metallic Wigner crystal phase, characterized by a finite ground state density of vacancies, we find phonons with a dispersion relation that varies in an unusual way with the vacancy density. Consequently, we discuss how thermodynamic properties of such a vacancy Fermi liquid can be probed in measurements of the melting temperature and speed of sound. The field theory developed here can serve as a starting point in the study of anomalous Hall crystals and charge density waves with defects.

</details>


### [94] [Manifest charge-transfer physics in T$^\prime$-La$_2$NiO$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19217)
*Wiktoria Lewandowski,Steffen Bötzel,Ilya M. Eremin,Frank Lechermann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理多体理论研究了T′-La₂NiO₄（具有Ni的平面正方形氧配位）这一假想化合物，发现它是一种典型的电荷转移绝缘体，带隙约1.8 eV，掺杂后空穴主要进入O(2p)态而电子进入Ni-d_{x²-y²}态，且化学计量比下不存在最近邻反铁磁序，与T结构形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受铜氧化物中La₂CuO₄存在T′结构多晶型的启发，研究人员希望探究镍氧化物La₂NiO₄在T′结构（Ni为平面正方形氧配位）下的电子结构和关联物理特性。

Method: 采用第一性原理多体理论（first-principles many-body theory）对假想化合物T′-La₂NiO₄进行理论计算研究。

Result: T′-La₂NiO₄表现为典型的电荷转移绝缘体，电荷带隙约1.8 eV，与铜氧化物类似；掺杂后呈现强载流子不对称性：空穴主要进入O(2p)衍生态，电子主要进入Ni-d_{x²-y²}态；化学计量比下不存在最近邻反铁磁有序，与T结构的强反铁磁性截然不同。

Conclusion: 该理论研究为层状镍酸盐中具有挑战性电荷转移特征的关联物理开辟了新途径，有待实验验证。

Abstract: The La$_2$NiO$_4$ compound stabilizes in nature in the so-called T structure with octahedral oxygen coordination of the Ni site. Motivated by a similarly existing polymorph for La$_2$CuO$_4$ in the cuprate system, we here study La$_2$NiO$_4$ in the T$^\prime$-structure with square-planar oxygen coordination of Ni by means of first-principles many-body theory. The hypothetical T$^\prime$-La$_2$NiO$_4$ compound turns out to be a manifest charge-transfer insulator with a cuprate-like charge gap of $\sim 1.8$ eV. Upon doping, a strong carrier asymmetry is revealed, i.e. while holes dominantly enter O$(2p)$-derived states, electrons may become itinerant majorly within effective Ni-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ states. Nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic ordering at stoichiometry appears absent in the T$^\prime$ structure, in stark contrast to the strong antiferromagnetism in the T structure. Our theoretical study opens up new pathways for correlation physics in layered nickelates with challenging charge-transfer signatures, awaiting experimental inquiries.

</details>


### [95] [Magnetic exchange interactions in the molecular orbital spin system NaV2O5 from a distributed moment point of view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19224)
*Claudio Garcia,Ilteris K. Turan,Swagata Acharya,Mark van Schilfgaarde,Jerome Jackson,Walter R L. Lambrecht*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算发现，NaV2O5中氧原子上的诱导磁矩与钒原子上的磁矩交换作用强度相当甚至更大，且对临界温度起关键作用，并预测了高能光学自旋波激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将NaV2O5描述为四分之一填充的梯子系统，电子自旋局域在梯级上，但34 K以上温度的自旋态尚不清晰，需从分布磁矩角度重新理解其磁性机制。

Method: 采用准粒子自洽GW能带结构，基于线性响应方法计算各原子磁位点（包括钒、钒酰氧、桥氧）之间的交换相互作用，构建经典海森堡哈密顿量并求解自旋波谱。

Result: 氧原子上诱导的小磁矩之间的交换作用与钒原子间的交换作用量级相当甚至更大；这些氧磁矩对系统临界温度有决定性影响；自旋波谱中存在高能光学支集体激发。

Conclusion: 氧原子上的诱导磁矩在NaV2O5的磁性行为中不可忽略，必须纳入分布磁矩模型才能正确描述该体系的交换相互作用和临界温度。

Abstract: The magnetism in NaV2O5 results from doping of the narrow split-off conduction band of V2O5, which becomes half-filled and leads to a Mott-insulating splitting of spin resolved bands with anti-ferromagnetic order of the spins along the zigzag chains. In the Pmmn structure the spin of this S = 1/2 system is equally shared between two vanadium atoms, residing in a molecular orbital type state. While below 34 K a charge disproportionation occurs into V4+ and V5+, the situation above this temperature is less clear and amounts to a fluctuating moment with equal probability of occupancy of each V. While traditionally described as a quarter-filled ladder system with electron spin localized on the rungs of the ladder, we here take a distributed moment approach in terms of the spin density lumped into individual atomic magnetic sites, including the small induced moments on the oxygen atoms. Exchange interactions are calculated between these sites using a linear response approach based on quasiparticle-self-consistent GW band structures. Surprisingly we find the exchange interactions between the small magnetic moments induced on the vanadyl and bridge oxygen sites to be of the same order of magnitude and even larger than the exchange interactions between vanadium atoms. Their role in the critical temperature is found to be crucial. The spin wave spectra obtained from this classical Heisenberg type Hamiltonian extracted from first-principles contains unusual optic spin wave type collective excitations of high energy.

</details>


### [96] [Symmetry-protected cubic-touching topological surface bands with tunable singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19265)
*Jingtian Shi,Taylor L. Hughes,Ivar Martin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类在三维拓扑晶体绝缘体中的拓扑表面能带，这些能带具有对称性保护的三次方阶能带接触。在对称性约束下，通过调节粒子-空穴不对称性和各向异性，能带色散可以在三次方色散、护城河带和多微型谷结构（含范霍夫奇点）之间连续变化。这产生了一系列可调的态密度奇点，范围从幂律发散到对数发散，为在拓扑表面态中工程化强关联物质相提供了一个多功能平台，并在角动量3/2电子的棱柱晶格格点模型中给出了实例实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑晶体绝缘体中具有高阶能带接触（三次方阶）的拓扑表面能带，以及通过调节对称性允许的参数实现可调的态密度奇点，从而为强关联物理提供新的平台。

Method: 基于对称性分析分类拓扑表面能带结构，引入粒子-空穴不对称性和各向异性调控能带色散，并在角动量3/2电子的棱柱晶格格点模型中进行具体实现验证。

Result: 发现能带色散可在三次方色散、护城河带和多微型谷结构之间连续变化，态密度奇点可从幂律发散连续调节到对数发散。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一类可调的拓扑表面态系统，为在拓扑体系中工程化强关联物质相（如非常规超导、量子临界性等）提供了新的多功能平台。

Abstract: We propose a class of topological surface bands in three-dimensional topological crystalline insulators that have symmetry-protected cubic-order band touching. Within the symmetry constraint, the band dispersion can continuously vary between cubic dispersion, moat band and multi-mini-valley structure with van Hove singularities by adjusting particle-hole asymmetry and anisotropy. Thus, there is a family of tunable density-of-state singularities ranging from power-law to logarithmic divergences. This offrs a versatile platform for engineering strongly correlated phases of matter in topological surface states. We provide an example realization in a prism-lattice tight-binding model of angular-momentum-3/2 electrons.

</details>


### [97] [Photon Correlation Spectroscopy as a Probe of Critical Fluctuations in Correlated Electron Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19295)
*Natasha Kiper,Bertrand Evrard,Yuya Shimazaki,Ido Schwartz,Martin Kroner,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Atac Imamoglu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了利用光子相关光谱法研究半导体莫尔材料（双层MoSe₂夹单层h-BN）中层间电子密度涨落动力学，在Ising型层赝自旋相变处观察到大量电子关联转移导致的光子聚束效应，为利用光子关联测量电子相变动力学临界指数开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征关联电子相之间的相变、提取其临界指数、识别其普适类是多体物理学的核心问题，需要发展新的实验方法。

Method: 采用光子相关光谱法，研究由两层MoSe₂中间夹单层h-BN间隔层组成的半导体莫尔材料，通过散射光子的二阶关联函数测量层间电子动力学。

Result: 在Ising型层赝自旋相变起始处，大量电子在层间关联转移导致激子共振散射光出现光子聚束效应。

Conclusion: 光子关联测量可作为获取电子相变动力学临界指数的新方法。

Abstract: Characterizing phase transitions between correlated electronic phases, extracting their critical exponents, and identifying their universality class are of central interest in many-body physics. Here, we propose and demonstrate that photon correlation spectroscopy can be used to gain insight into the nature of critical electronic density fluctuations. We study a semiconductor moiré material consisting of two MoSe2 layers separated by a monolayer h-BN spacer and measure interlayer electron dynamics via the second-order correlation function of the scattered photons. The correlated transfer of large numbers of electrons between the layers at the onset of an Ising-type layer pseudo-spin phase transition leads to photon bunching in light scattered by the exciton resonance of one layer. Our measurements pave the way for using photon correlations as a method to access dynamical exponents associated with electronic phase transitions.

</details>

### [98] [Fibrations in Oriented Category Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18418)
*David Gepner,Hadrian Heine*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文从定向范畴论的视角研究高阶范畴的纤维化，给出了(∞,∞)-范畴和定向范畴纤维化的多种等价刻画，证明了纤维化范畴自然地构成定向范畴，并构建了高阶版本的自由纤维化、普遍纤维化以及Grothendieck构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在更高阶范畴论中，纤维化是重要的结构概念，但已有框架在处理lax现象时存在局限。本文引入定向范畴论（基于Gray张量积的丰富化框架）来统一和推广纤维化理论，旨在为(∞,∞)-范畴和定向范畴提供系统的纤维化刻画及其Grothendieck对应。

Method: 利用定向范畴论这一框架，通过系统地在Gray张量积上进行丰富化来刻画lax现象；给出纤维化的多种等价刻画；研究纤维化与定向拉回之间的相互作用；构建高阶自由纤维化和普遍纤维化，进而推导出Grothendieck构造。

Result: 得到了(∞,∞)-范畴和定向范畴纤维化的多个等价刻画；证明了纤维化范畴自然地形成定向范畴；建立了高阶自由纤维化、普遍纤维化以及相应的Grothendieck构造。

Conclusion: 定向范畴论为理解和处理高阶范畴的纤维化提供了统一且自然的框架，成功将经典的Grothendieck构造推广到(∞,∞)-范畴和定向范畴的情形。

Abstract: We study fibrations of higher categories from the perspective of oriented category theory, a framework which accounts for lax phenomena in higher category theory via systematic enrichment in the Gray tensor product. We give several equivalent characterizations of fibrations of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories and oriented categories, and show that categories of fibrations naturally organize to form oriented categories. We study the interaction between fibrations and oriented pullbacks and construct higher-categorical versions of free fibrations and universal fibrations. The latter give rise to Grothendieck constructions for fibrations of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories and oriented categories.

</details>


### [99] [Level structures and cyclic power operations on the homology of $\mathbb{E}_\infty$ spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18670)
*Catherine Li*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文讨论了如何通过代数几何理解周期复配边（periodic complex bordism）的循环幂运算，该理论源于Ando、Hopkins和Strickland发展的Morava E-理论幂运算。利用这一视角，描述了在E是2-周期偶E_∞环时，如何计算BU和BU×ℤ上E-同调的幂运算，并在p=2下给出了具体例子，特别是计算了加法运算Δ在不可分解元Q̂(E₀(BU))上的作用，其中E=KU, E₂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将循环幂运算与代数几何联系起来，扩展幂运算理论在周期复配边领域的应用，并通过具体计算验证理论框架的有效性。

Method: 基于Ando-Hopkins-Strickland的Morava E-理论幂运算，通过代数几何视角建立周期复配边的循环幂运算理论。对2-周期偶E_∞环E，计算BU和BU×ℤ上E-同调的幂运算，并在p=2时进行显式计算。

Result: 在p=2下，具体计算了加法运算Δ在不可分解元Q̂(E₀(BU))上的作用，其中E取KU和E₂两种情形。

Conclusion: 通过代数几何方法成功描述了周期复配边的循环幂运算，并在p=2时给出了显式计算结果，验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: In this document, we discuss how cyclic power operations for periodic complex bordism can be understood through algebraic geometry, following from the theory of power operations for Morava $E$-theory developed by Ando, Hopkins, and Strickland. Using this perspective, we describe how one computes power operations on the $E$-homology of $BU$ and $BU\times \mathbb{Z}$, where $E$ is a 2-periodic even $\mathbb{E}_\infty$ ring. Then we provide some explicit example computations at $p=2$; in particular, we compute the action of the additive operations $Δ$ on the indecomposables $\widehat{Q}(E_0(BU))$ for $E=KU, E_2$.

</details>


### [100] [Geometry-Induced Hodge Stars on Rips and Dowker--Rips Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18692)
*Jiahui Chen,Sebastian Wilcox*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文针对Rips类型复形（Vietoris–Rips复形、Dowker复形及Dowker–Rips变体）缺乏规范的对偶胞结构（即离散外微积分Hodge星算子所需的关键要素）的问题，提出了通过引入几何诱导的对角Hodge星算子（表示为单纯形正权重）来弥补这一缺陷。作者证明了任意正对角权重都能定义加权Hodge拉普拉斯算子，其核维数等于复形的Betti数，而非零谱由几何权重决定。文章重点设计了两种可计算的权重方案：基于欧几里得单纯形体体积的单纯形体积权重，以及基于Dowker风格支持函数的软见证权重。论文证明了权重的正定性、加权自伴性及Betti数保持性质，建立了软见证支持的渐近衰减率刻画，并描述了用于比较Rips复形上几何感知Hodge谱的谱描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Rips类型复形（VR复形、Dowker复形及其flagified变体）在拓扑数据分析中虽能通过成对信息编码拓扑，但在固定尺度下几乎不保留底层几何信息。与带有规范对偶胞结构的Alpha复形或网格离散化不同，Rips类型复形缺乏离散外微积分中Hodge星算子所需的原始-对偶胞结构，从而无法自然编码度量信息。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过引入几何诱导的Hodge星算子来赋予Rips类型复形度量感知能力。

Method: 论文提出为Rips类型复形K配备几何诱导的对角Hodge星算子，表示为正单纯形权重W_k=diag{w_k(σ): σ∈K_k}，这些权重定义了k-上链上的加权内积。由此产生的加权离散Hodge拉普拉斯算子Δ_k^W的核维数等于底层复形的第k个Betti数，而非零谱由几何权重控制。文章重点设计了两种可计算的权重方案：(1) 单纯形体积权重，基于欧几里得单纯形的体积；(2) 软见证权重，基于Dowker风格的支持函数s_t(σ;Y)，该函数量化了flagification过程中丢失的高阶见证支持。论文证明了任意正对角权重下权重的正定性、加权自伴性及Betti数保持性质，建立了软见证支持的渐近衰减率刻画，并描述了从Δ_k^W导出的用于比较Rips复形上几何感知Hodge谱的谱描述符。

Result: 论文证明了：(1) 任意正对角权重都能定义加权离散Hodge拉普拉斯算子，其核维数等于复形的Betti数；(2) 单纯形体积权重和软见证权重两种方案均满足正定性、加权自伴性及Betti数保持性质；(3) 建立了软见证支持函数s_t(σ;Y)的渐近衰减率刻画；(4) 描述了从加权Hodge Laplacian导出的谱描述符，用于比较不同Rips复形上的几何感知Hodge谱。

Conclusion: 本文成功为Rips类型复形引入了几何诱导的Hodge星算子，解决了其缺乏规范对偶胞结构的问题。通过单纯形体积权重和软见证权重两种方案，使得Rips复形能够编码几何信息，同时保持拓扑不变性（Betti数）。加权Hodge Laplacian的构建不仅保留了核维数等于Betti数的关键性质，还使得非零谱能够反映几何信息，为拓扑数据分析中结合度量和拓扑信息提供了新的工具。软见证权重的渐近衰减刻画进一步揭示了flagification过程中信息损失的量化特征。

Abstract: The Vietoris--Rips complex $\mathrm{VR}_ε(X)$, the Dowker complex $\mathrm{D}_R(X,Y)$, and its flagified Dowker--Rips variant $\mathrm{DR}_R(X,Y)=\mathrm{F}(\mathrm{D}_R(X,Y))$ are simplicial complexes constructed from metric data or witness relations. They are useful in topological data analysis because they encode topology through combinatorial data derived from pairwise information, but at a fixed scale they retain little of the underlying geometry. Unlike alpha complexes or mesh-based discretizations, Rips-type complexes carry no canonical primal--dual cell structure, which is the ingredient used by the discrete exterior calculus Hodge star to encode metric information. We address this gap by equipping a Rips-type complex $K$ with diagonal geometry-induced Hodge stars represented by positive simplex weights $W_k=\operatorname{diag}\{w_k(σ):σ\in K_k\}$, which define weighted inner products on $k$-cochains. The resulting weighted discrete Hodge Laplacian $Δ_k^W$ has kernel dimension equal to the $k$th Betti number of the underlying complex, while its nonzero spectrum is governed by the chosen geometric weights. The central issue is therefore not the existence of a weighted Laplacian, since any positive diagonal weights define one, but the design of weights that encode meaningful metric or witness geometry. We focus on two computable choices: simplex-volume weights, based on Euclidean simplex volumes, and soft witness weights, based on a Dowker-style support function $s_t(σ;Y)$ that quantifies higher-order witness support lost under flagification. We prove positivity, weighted self-adjointness, and Betti-number preservation for arbitrary positive diagonal weights, establish an asymptotic decay-rate characterization for soft witness support, and describe spectral descriptors derived from $Δ_k^W$ for comparing geometry-aware Hodge spectra on Rips complexes.

</details>


### [101] [On the toric lifting properties for simplicial $3$-spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18706)
*Suyoung Choi,Yuanxin Guan,Hyeontae Jang,Zhi Lü*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了模2特征映射在单纯3-球面上的提升问题，利用坏块分割证明了可提升性的避让准则，展示了至多20个顶点的单纯3-球面具有环面提升性质，并得到了像大小和联合类型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单纯3-球面上模2特征映射的提升问题，这是代数拓扑与组合拓扑中的经典问题，与环面拓扑和矩角复形理论密切相关。

Method: 利用万有复形X(Z₂⁴)的坏块分割技术，建立可提升性的避让准则。通过组合与拓扑方法分析顶点数与提升性质的关系。

Result: 证明了至多20个顶点的单纯3-球面都具有环面提升性质；得到了像大小和联合类型结果；验证了万有复形意义下像大小界限的紧致性。

Conclusion: 建立了单纯3-球面上模2特征映射提升问题的避让准则，给出了顶点数上限20的充分条件，并证明了相关界限的最优性。

Abstract: We study the lifting problem for mod $2$ characteristic maps over simplicial $3$-spheres. Using a bad-block partition of the universal complex $X(\mathbb{Z}_2^4)$, we prove an avoidance criterion for liftability. We show that every simplicial $3$-sphere with at most $20$ vertices has the toric lifting property. We also obtain image-size and join-type results, and prove sharpness of the image-size bound in the universal-complex sense.

</details>


### [102] [Topology of the orbit space of a maximal torus action on $\operatorname{Gr}(2,\mathbb{C}^{5})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19025)
*Shahryar Ghaed Sharaf*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文采用拓扑方法，引入了$\operatorname{Gr}(2, \mathbb{C}^{5})$上最大环面作用轨道空间的同伦模型，计算了其整系数同调群，并通过CW复形研究了其同伦类型，确定了顶胞腔的附着映射的同伦类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用拓扑学方法（同伦模型）研究复格拉斯曼流形$\operatorname{Gr}(2, \mathbb{C}^{5})$上最大环面作用的轨道空间的拓扑性质。

Method: 引入轨道空间的同伦模型，赋予该模型一个包含一个顶胞腔的CW复形结构，确定顶胞腔附着映射的同伦类。

Result: 计算了轨道空间的整系数同调群，研究了其同伦类型，确定了顶胞腔附着映射的同伦类。

Conclusion: 通过同伦模型有效刻画了该轨道空间的拓扑结构，为理解复格拉斯曼流形上环面作用的轨道空间提供了同伦论层面的描述。

Abstract: In this work, we take a topological approach and introduce a homotopy model of the orbit space of a maximal torus action on $\operatorname{Gr}(2, \mathbb{C}^{5})$. Using this homotopy model, we compute the integral homology groups and study the homotopy type of the orbit space. More precisely, we endow the homotopy model with a CW complex that contains one top cell and determine the homotopy class of the attaching map of the top cell of the orbit space.

</details>

### [103] [Approximating SPR Distance Between Phylogenetic Trees with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18311)
*Renata Martins Castanheira,Miguel Bugalho,Cátia Vaz*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用图神经网络（GIN）在近常数时间内近似计算系统发育树之间的子树修剪与重接（SPR）距离。作者构建了包含864棵系统发育树和388个标注树对的数据集，建立了可复现的预处理流程，验证了未生根启发式距离与精确生根SPR距离的高度相关性（Pearson 0.98-0.99），并训练了孪生图同构网络回归器。在分布内数据上，模型解释了约87-90%的方差（R²≈0.87-0.90），误差比均值基线低约4倍；但向更大规模树的外推能力有限，准确率会下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育树拓扑比较对理解流行病动态至关重要，但SPR距离等有生物学意义的距离度量是NP-hard问题，在大规模数据集上难以计算。因此需要寻找能在近常数时间内近似SPR距离的方法。

Method: (1) 构建了包含864棵系统发育树（UPGMA和Neighbor-Joining推断，覆盖4种细菌物种，多达9,500个分离株）和388个标注树对的数据集；(2) 建立了可复现的预处理流程，包括中点重新定根；(3) 验证了phangorn::SPR.dist未生根启发式与rspr精确生根距离的高度相关性；(4) 训练了孪生图同构网络（Siamese GIN）回归器来近似SPR距离。

Result: 分布内数据上，模型R²≈0.87（保留集）和0.90±0.19（分层交叉验证），误差比均值基线低约4倍；跨物种迁移时R²≈0.37；向更大规模树外推时准确率下降。

Conclusion: 图神经网络可以在分布内有效近似SPR距离，但外推能力有限。公开的数据集和验证的启发式与精确距离关系为可扩展的学习型SPR近似方法提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Comparing phylogenetic tree topologies is essential for understanding epidemic dynamics, yet biologically meaningful distances such as the Subtree Prune and Regraft (SPR) distance are NP-hard to compute and intractable on large datasets. We investigate whether a Graph Neural Network (GNN) can approximate SPR distances in near-constant time per comparison after training. Our contributions are fourfold. First, we build and publicly release a dataset of 864 phylogenetic trees inferred with UPGMA and Neighbor-Joining over four bacterial species, spanning up to 9{,}500 isolates, together with 388 labelled tree pairs. Second, we establish a reproducible pre-processing pipeline including midpoint re-rooting, which reduces tree depth and supplies the rooting required for exact distance computation and for the model's root-based features. Third, we validate the supervision target: on small trees, where exact SPR is tractable, the unrooted phangorn::SPR.dist heuristic correlates almost perfectly with the exact rooted distance computed by rspr (Pearson $0.98$--$0.99$), making it an excellent monotonic surrogate. Lastly, we train a Siamese Graph Isomorphism Network (GIN) regressor. In-distribution, i.e., held-out trees from the same species and size range as training, it explains roughly 87--90% of the variance ($R^2 \approx 0.87$ on a held-out split; $0.90 \pm 0.19$ under stratified cross-validation), with about four times lower error than a mean-predictor baseline, and shows partial transfer to unseen species ($R^2 \approx 0.37$). Its main limitation is extrapolation to trees larger than those seen in training, where accuracy collapses. The released dataset and the validated heuristic versus exact relationship provide a reproducible basis for scaling learned SPR approximation.

</details>


### [104] [Evaluating the Impact of Epidemic Control via State-Dependent Markovian Switching Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18364)
*Vasileios E. Papageorgiou,Irene Votsi,Samis Trevezas*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在有限人群中、干预机制间马尔可夫切换的SIR流行病随机模型。通过引入相位分量，允许传播、恢复和免疫获得率依赖于活跃的干预机制，并利用易感人群的单调性推导了灭绝时间与感染数量的联合变换递推关系。以卢森堡猴痘数据为例，比较了固定控制、早期vs延迟干预、疫苗接种和状态依赖升级等策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病模型通常假设干预机制固定不变，但实际疫情中政策会动态调整。需要一种能够精确刻画干预机制切换对流行病随机演化影响的数学框架，以进行条件性模型比较。

Method: 采用有限相位分量增强SIR状态空间，允许传播率、恢复率和直接免疫获得率随活跃干预机制变化；相位转换强度可依赖于当前感染人数；利用易感人群的单调性推导灭绝时间和感染数量的联合拉普拉斯-斯蒂尔切斯变换和概率生成函数的水平递推关系，将大规模全局线性系统转化为小规模相位级别求解。

Result: 基于卢森堡猴痘周发病率数据，通过最大似然法标定单相SIR基线模型，并在该基线上进行条件性比较，展示了切换机制如何影响总感染人数和灭绝时间及其离散程度。

Conclusion: 切换机制显著影响疫情结局（感染总数和灭绝时间）及其分布离散性。本文提供的是基于模型的条件性比较，而非对卢森堡历史干预效果的真实估计。

Abstract: We develop an exact finite-population stochastic framework for SIR epidemics evolving under Markovian switching between intervention regimes. The epidemic state is augmented by a finite phase component, allowing transmission, recovery, and direct immunity-acquisition rates to depend on the active regime. Phase-transition intensities may depend on the current epidemic state, so that policy escalation can react to the number of infectious individuals. Exploiting the monotonicity of the susceptible compartment, we derive level-wise recursions for the joint Laplace--Stieltjes transform and probability generating function of the extinction time and the number of infections generated before extinction. These recursions yield the infection-count distribution, conditional extinction-time transforms, and mixed moments linking epidemic duration and infection burden, while replacing a large global linear system with small phase-level solves. The framework is illustrated using weekly mpox incidence data from Luxembourg. A baseline one-phase SIR model is calibrated by maximum likelihood under a Poisson observation model. The calibrated baseline is then used for conditional comparisons of fixed control regimes, early versus delayed strict intervention, vaccination-supported control, and state-dependent escalation. The results show how switching mechanisms affect both the total number of infected individuals and the extinction time, including their dispersion. Since the switching mechanisms are specified rather than estimated from the intervention history, the results are conditional model-based comparisons rather than estimates of the historical effects of interventions in Luxembourg.

</details>


### [105] [Gravity-Driven Eco-Epidemiological Dynamics in Tri-Trophic Food Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18434)
*Yuvrajsingh Pravinsingh Patil,Eckehard Schöll,Fakhteh Ghanbarnejad,Chandrakala Meena*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于引力模型的生态流行病学框架，研究三营养级食物链中空间介导的营养相互作用与传染病如何共同影响生态系统稳定性。研究发现，中间营养级感染通过Hopf分岔引发持续振荡，而顶级捕食者感染导致种群灭绝，引力耦合强度调节生态阈值，感染的营养位置决定转变性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对空间介导的营养相互作用如何影响疾病驱动的生态系统动态理解不足，缺乏统一的理论框架来阐明营养相互作用与传染病共同调控生态系统稳定性的机制。

Method: 开发了一个基于引力模型的生态流行病学框架，用于三营养级食物链系统，其中营养相互作用依赖于物种丰度和有效相互作用距离；通过系统变化引力耦合强度，分析不同营养级感染下的动力学行为（Hopf分岔、稳定振荡、种群灭绝）。

Result: 1) 无病食物链系统存在稳定共存平衡点；2) 中间营养级感染通过Hopf分岔导致持续振荡；3) 顶级捕食者感染导致从持续到灭绝的定性转变；4) 引力耦合强度调节生态阈值，感染的营养位置决定转变性质。

Conclusion: 建立了理解空间介导的营养相互作用与传染病共同调控生态系统稳定性的统一框架，为疾病驱动的生态群落动态提供了新见解。

Abstract: Ecological communities are shaped by the interplay between trophic interactions and infectious disease, yet how spatially mediated interactions influence disease-driven ecosystem dynamics remains poorly understood. Here, we develop a gravity-based eco-epidemiological framework for a tri-trophic food chain in which trophic interaction depends on species abundances and effective interaction distance. The disease-free food chain system supports a stable coexistence equilibrium, providing a baseline for investigating disease-induced ecological transitions. Introducing infection at the intermediate trophic level destabilizes this equilibrium through a Hopf bifurcation, leading to sustained oscillations, whereas infection at the top predator level results in a qualitatively different transition from persistence to extinction. By systematically varying the gravity coupling strength, we show that gravity-mediated trophic interactions regulate the thresholds separating these ecological regimes, while the trophic position of infection determines the nature of the transition. Together, these findings establish a unified framework for understanding how spatially mediated trophic interactions and infectious disease jointly govern ecosystem stability, providing new insights into disease-driven dynamics in ecological communities.

</details>


### [106] [Additional Food Enhances the Bifurcation Structure of Predator Competition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18613)
*Kanishka Goyal,Chanaka Kottegoda,Urvashi Verma,Rana D. Parshad*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有广义捕食者竞争和Holling II型功能反应的附加食物模型，发现系统可存在codimension至少为4的Bogdanov-Takens分岔、codimension为3的全局Hopf分岔和同宿分岔，并揭示附加食物能增强Bazykin类型模型的分岔结构。结果对大豆蚜虫的综合害虫管理具有应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管附加食物模型和捕食者竞争模型各自已被广泛研究，但将二者统一建模后的全局分岔结构尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对带有广义捕食者竞争和Holling type-II功能反应的附加食物模型进行动力学分析，通过分岔理论（Bogdanov-Takens分岔、Hopf分岔、同宿分岔）研究系统的平衡点及极限环结构。

Result: 系统最多可存在三个内部平衡点；展示cusp型（或focus型）Bogdanov-Takens分岔（codimension至少4或3）、codimension为3的全局Hopf分岔和同宿分岔；可在BT点附近存在三个极限环。

Conclusion: 附加食物能够增强Bazykin类型模型的分岔结构；结合北美中北部大豆蚜虫的长期野外数据（显示两个不同的极限环），说明附加食物的生物控制是治理入侵性害虫的有效管理策略。

Abstract: Additional food sources and predator competition are both known to impact the dynamics of predator-prey models. The Bazykin model of predator competition, with Holling type II functional response, possesses a rich bifurcation structure consisting of a focus-type degenerate Bogdanov-Takens bifurcation of codimension 3, and a degenerate Hopf bifurcation of codimension at most 2. Additional food models on the other hand are able to drive pest populations lower, with vast applicability in biological control. Despite these models being studied rigorously in the literature, the global bifurcation structure, of their possible complex dynamics, in a unified model, is unknown. In this work, we study an additional food model with generalized predator competition and Holling type-II functional response. Depending on the parameter values, the system can have up to three interior equilibria. Further, we show that this system exhibits a cusp-type (or focus-type) Bogdanov-Takens bifurcation of codimension at least 4 (or 3), a global Hopf bifurcation of codimension 3, and a homoclinic bifurcation of codimension 3. This shows there could exist three limit cycles around the BT point. These results demonstrate that additional food in Bazykin type models, can enhance their bifurcation structure. We discuss the applicability of these results to integrated pest management programs for the soybean aphid, wherein long term field data in the North-Central United States, shows two distinct limit cycles in aphid populations and their predators. Our results suggest biological control with additional food, is an effective management tactic for invasive pests.

</details>


### [107] [Ecological networks of viable species with degree-dependent interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18809)
*Hae Seong Lee,Deok-Sun Lee,Sang Hoon Lee,Samir Suweis,Hye Jin Park*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文在广义Lotka-Volterra框架中引入度依赖性相互作用强度，发现了两种不同的相态：枢纽有利相（高度连接物种优先存活）和枢纽抑制相（高度连接物种面临更高灭绝风险），并推导出相边界。这些相态通过对相互作用的选择性移除，改变了存活物种网络的度-度相关性（同配性），为解释真实生态网络中广泛分布的同配性值提供了机制基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的生态群落中，物种的相互作用强度通常与其连接度（度）存在相关性，但现有的GLV生态网络模型通常假设所有连接的相互作用强度服从同一分布，忽略了这一特征。

Method: 将度依赖性相互作用强度引入广义随机Lotka-Volterra模型，利用动态平均场理论进行系统分析。

Result: 识别出两种相态：枢纽有利相（高度连接物种优先存活）和枢纽抑制相（高度连接物种面临更高灭绝风险）；推导出相边界的解析表达式；发现这些相态通过选择性移除特定相互作用改变存活物种网络的同配性。

Conclusion: 度依赖性相互作用是塑造物种存活模式以及重现真实生态网络中同配性多样性的基本机制。

Abstract: The generalized Lotka-Volterra (GLV) framework, recently advanced by dynamical mean-field theory, enables the systematic analysis of large ecological networks. When combined with structured interaction topologies, previous studies have shown that the viability of species, defined by having a positive stationary abundance, depends on the number of their interacting neighbors or the "degree." While such model studies usually assume that interaction strengths follow an identical distribution across all connected pairs, real ecological communities often exhibit correlations between interaction strength and a species' degree. To capture this overlooked feature, we introduce degree-dependent interaction strengths into a generalized random LV model. We identify two distinct regimes: a hub-favored phase, where highly connected species survive preferentially, and a hub-suppressed phase, where they face higher extinction risks. We analytically derive the phase boundary where these degree-dependent strengths precisely balance the connectivity effect, leaving all species equally susceptible. Crucially, these phases induce opposing shifts in the degree-degree correlation or "assortativity" of the network of viable species: the hub-favored phase enhances disassortativity by selectively removing the interactions between low-degree species, whereas the hub-suppressed phase reduces it as the interactions involving hub species tend to disappear. Ultimately, our findings demonstrate that degree-dependent interactions are a fundamental mechanism not only for shaping species survival, but for naturally reproducing the wide range of assortativity values observed in real ecological networks.

</details>


### [108] [Evolutionary Entropy Shapes Lifespan of the Greenland Shar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19203)
*Jorge Buescu,Saber N. Elaidy,Henrique M. Oliveira*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究应用进化熵理论分析格陵兰鲨（最长寿脊椎动物，雌性约156年性成熟，最大年龄近400年）的生殖窗口。通过开放群体的齐次性和临界阈值定理，推导出生殖场景：临界阈值q_c≈0.9763（近统一持续状态），世代时间接近或超过两个世纪，生殖分位数延伸至第三至第五世纪，与已知最老龄格陵兰鲨年龄范围吻合。临界阈值方程具有变分解释，其唯一解即进化熵最大化时的q值，熵分解logλ=H+Φ进一步将结果置于生活史熵理论框架中。该方法可为种群数据匮乏的物种提供生殖窗口的定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究极端长寿（如格陵兰鲨寿命近400年）的生殖策略与人口统计特征之间的兼容性，理解何种生殖窗口能与极端长寿表型共存。

Method: 应用年龄结构种群的进化熵理论，利用开放群体的齐次性定理和临界阈值定理，推导世代时间、生殖分位数和熵最大化的生殖端点。通过变分法解释临界阈值方程，并利用熵分解logλ=H+Φ将结果置于生活史熵理论框架中。

Result: 临界阈值q_c≈0.9763（近统一持续状态），世代时间接近或超过两世纪，生殖分位数延伸至第三、第四和第五世纪，与最老龄格陵兰鲨年龄范围一致。临界阈值方程的唯一解对应进化熵最大化的q值，熵分解给出了与校准的生殖持续状态兼容的Perron增长率上界。

Conclusion: 进化熵理论可为数据匮乏的物种提供生殖窗口的定量约束，该方法适用于缺乏详细种群数据但具有极端长寿特征的物种生殖策略分析。

Abstract: The Greenland shark (\emph{Somniosus microcephalus}) is among the longest-lived vertebrates known, with female maturity estimated at approximately 156 years and maximum ages approaching four centuries. We apply the theory of evolutionary entropy for age-structured populations to investigate which reproductive windows are compatible with such an extreme demographic profile. Using the homogeneity and critical-threshold theorems for open-group populations, we derive demographic scenarios for generation time, reproductive quantiles, and entropy-maximizing reproductive endpoints. The critical threshold occurs at $q_c\approx0.9763$, in a near-unity persistence regime. Longevity-calibrated scenarios span the boundary separating finite-maximum and asymptotic entropy regimes. These scenarios lead to generation times approaching or exceeding two centuries and reproductive quantiles extending through the third, fourth, and fifth centuries of life, encompassing the age range reported for the oldest Greenland sharks. The critical-threshold equation admits a variational interpretation: its unique solution is precisely the value of $q$ at which evolutionary entropy is maximized. The entropic decomposition $\logλ=H+Φ$ further places these results within the entropic theory of life histories and yields a simple upper bound on the Perron growth rate compatible with the calibrated reproductive persistence. These methods may be applied to obtain quantitative constraints on reproductive windows in species with largely unknown demographic data.

</details>

### [109] [On a certain arithmetic function defined via Bernoulli numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18607)
*Andrei R. Svinin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个基于伯努利数在奇数上定义的算术函数的整性性质，发现该函数将素数、卡迈克尔数和Giuga数统一为一个整性判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索伯努利数构造的算术函数在奇数集上的整性性质，并揭示其与三类特殊数（素数、卡迈克尔数、Giuga数）之间的内在联系。

Method: 在奇数 n≥3 上定义一个基于伯努利数的算术函数，通过分析其整性条件来刻画数的性质。

Result: 该函数将素数、卡迈克尔数和Giuga数这三类不同的数类统一到同一个整性判别准则之下。

Conclusion: 本文给出的整性判别法为三类经典数类提供了统一的视角，且文章初等，适合广泛读者群。

Abstract: We investigate the integrality property of an arithmetic function defined on the set of odd numbers $n\geq 3$. It is constructed via Bernoulli numbers. As a result, we show that this function unifies three distinct number classes -- primes, Carmichael numbers, and Giuga numbers -- into a single integrality criterion. The article is elementary and therefore accessible to a broad audience.

</details>


### [110] [Binary quadratic forms and elliptic curves with analytic rank one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18728)
*Tong Wei,Shuai Zhai*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆曲线族 $dy^2 = f(x)$ 的解析秩，证明了存在无穷多个属于二次型 $Q$ 所在属中二次型所表示集合中的 $d$，使得该扭曲椭圆曲线具有解析秩一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扭曲椭圆曲线解析秩的分布规律，特别是对于由给定二元二次型 $Q$ 所在属中形式所表示的整数 $d$，能否保证相应的扭曲曲线具有解析秩一。

Method: 利用椭圆曲线的 Weierstrass 方程 $y^2 = f(x)$ 与正定二元二次型 $Q$，通过分析二次型属中形式的表示性质，结合解析数论和椭圆曲线理论的方法，证明存在无穷多个 $d$ 使得扭曲曲线 $dy^2 = f(x)$ 的解析秩为一。

Result: 证明了存在无穷多个 $d$，其属于 $Q$ 所在属中二次型所表示的整数集合，使得扭曲椭圆曲线 $dy^2 = f(x)$ 具有解析秩一。

Conclusion: 文章建立了二次型的属与椭圆曲线解析秩之间的深刻联系，为扭曲椭圆曲线的秩分布提供了新的无穷性结果。

Abstract: Given an elliptic curve with Weierstrass equation $y^2=f(x)$, and a positive definite binary quadratic form $Q(u, v)$. We show that there are infinitely many $d$ in the set represented by the quadratic forms in the genus of $Q$ such that the twisted elliptic curve $dy^2=f(x)$ has analytic rank one.

</details>


### [111] [Divisor moments of polynomials in Fourier coefficients of modular forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18832)
*Wonwoong Lee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究非CM新形式傅里叶系数多项式表达式中除数函数的高阶矩。估计中对数指数仅依赖于多项式的不可约因子个数，且在佐藤-泰特定理约束下保持不变。证明结合了有效的切博塔廖夫定理（或有效的切博塔廖夫-佐藤-泰特定理）、联合循环类型的算术以及具有Frobenius支撑的多变量乘性函数的均值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非CM新形式傅里叶系数多项式表达式中除数函数的高阶矩，探索对数指数与多项式不可约因子个数之间的关系，以及佐藤-泰特定理约束下的稳定性。

Method: 结合有效的切博塔廖夫定理（或有效的切博塔廖夫-佐藤-泰特定理）、联合循环类型的算术以及具有Frobenius支撑的多变量乘性函数的均值估计。

Result: 所得估计中对数指数仅依赖于多项式的不可约因子个数，且在佐藤-泰特定理约束下保持不变。

Conclusion: 通过结合代数数论和解析数论工具，成功建立了非CM新形式傅里叶系数相关除数函数高阶矩的精确估计。

Abstract: We study higher moments of the divisor function evaluated at polynomial expressions in the Fourier coefficients of a non-CM newform. The logarithmic exponent appearing in our estimates depends only on the number of irreducible factors of the polynomial and remains unchanged under a Sato--Tate restriction. The proof combines an effective Chebotarev theorem, or an effective Chebotarev--Sato--Tate theorem, with the arithmetic of joint cycle types and a mean value estimation for multivariable multiplicative functions with Frobenian support.

</details>


### [112] [On Diophantine $m$-tuples related to primitive elements of finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18896)
*Hai-Liang Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上与本原元相关的Diophantine数组，给出了$\mathcal{P}$-Diophantine $m$-元组数量的渐近公式，并证明了存在性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受有限域上Diophantine数组近期工作的启发，本文考虑与本原元相关的Diophantine数组，旨在揭示此类数组的计数规律和存在条件。

Method: 通过数论和有限域理论，利用Euler totient函数，采用渐近分析方法推导$\mathcal{P}$-Diophantine $m$-元组数量$N_m$的渐近公式，并基于指数条件证明存在性。

Result: 得到渐近公式$m!\cdot N_m=\left(\frac{\varphi(q-1)}{q-1}\right)^{m(m+1)/2}q^m+O_{m,r}\left(q^{m-\frac{1}{2}+r}\right)$，并证明当$q\ge \exp(\exp(m(m+1)))$时存在$\mathcal{P}$-Diophantine $m$-元组。

Conclusion: 本文建立了有限域上与本原元相关的Diophantine数组的渐近计数理论，并为这类数组的存在性提供了充分条件。

Abstract: Inspired by recent works on Diophantine tuples over finite fields, in this paper we consider Diophantine tuples related to primitive elements of finite fields. Let $\mathbb{F}_q$ be the finite field with $q$ elements and $\mathbb{F}_q^*=\mathbb{F}_q\setminus\{0\}$ be the multiplicative cyclic group of all non-zero elements over $\mathbb{F}_q$. An element $g\in\mathbb{F}_q$ is called primitive if $g$ generates the group $\mathbb{F}_q^*$. A set $\{x_1,x_2,\cdots,x_m\}\subseteq\mathbb{F}_q^*$ of $m$ elements is said to be a $\mathcal{P}$-Diophantine $m$-tuple over $\mathbb{F}_q$ if $x_ix_j+1$ is primitive for any $1\le i\le j\le m$. Let $N_m$ denote the number of $\mathcal{P}$-Diophantine tuples over $\mathbb{F}_q$. Then we obtain the asymptotic formula
  $$m!\cdot N_m=\left(\frac{\varphi(q-1)}{q-1}\right)^{m(m+1)/2}q^m+O_{m,r}\left(q^{m-\frac{1}{2}+r}\right),$$
  where $\varphi(\cdot)$ is the Euler totient function and $r\in(0, 1/2)$ is an arbitrary real number. Moreover, we prove that there exists a $\mathcal{P}$-Diophantine $m$-tuple over $\mathbb{F}_q$ whenever $q\ge \exp(\exp(m(m+1)))$.

</details>


### [113] [On the digits of the sum of proper divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18981)
*Kübra Benli,Cécile Dartyge,Charlotte Dombrowsky,Paul Pollack,Lola Thompson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究整数真因子和函数 s(n) 的数字概率性质，证明 s(n) 在对数密度意义下服从本福特定律，几乎所有 n 的前后 k(x) 位数字包含所有十进制数字，并给出合数 n 中 s(n) 缺失数字的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索真因子和函数 s(n) 的数字分布规律，特别是与 Benford 定律的关系；并澄清一个近期结果中素数输入对缺失数字集合原像的显著贡献。

Method: 运用对数密度分析概率性质；通过构造和估计证明数字出现规律；采用上界估计方法处理合数情形，并与先前工作的素数贡献进行对比。

Result: s(n) 在对数密度下服从 Benford 定律；几乎所有 n 的前后 k(x) 位均包含所有十进制数字；合数 n 中 s(n) 缺失数字的计数远小于包含素数的情况。

Conclusion: 素数输入对 s^{-1}(A) 的贡献占主导地位，排除素数后计数显著减小，揭示了素数在数字分布问题中的特殊作用。

Abstract: We study several probabilistic questions concerning the digits of $s(n)$, the sum of proper divisors of an integer $n$. In particular, we show that $s(n)$ obeys Benford's law with respect to logarithmic density. Moreover, we show that, for every function $k(x) \rightarrow \infty$, almost all integers $n \leq x$ have every decimal digit occurring among the first $k(x)$ digits and the last $k(x)$ digits of $s(n)$.
  We also present an upper bound for the number of composite integers $n$ up to $x$ for which $s(n)$ is missing at least one digit in its decimal expansion. This is in contrast with the main result of a recent paper of Benli, Cesana, Dartyge, Dombrowsky, and Thompson, in which the inputs $n$ were not required to be composite. It turns out that the primes make a substantial contribution to the preimage set $s^{-1}(\mathcal{A})$, where $\mathcal{A}$ is a set of integers with missing digits. Our result for composite $n$ shows that the count is much smaller when prime inputs are excluded.

</details>


### [114] [A Distinct Covering System with Minimum Modulus 7 and Minimal Least Common Multiple 10080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19029)
*Jiheng Zhang,Shiliang Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了最小模数为7的不同覆盖系统的最小可能最小公倍数。Klein曾构造出一个LCM为15120的此类系统并猜想其最小，而本文给出了一个LCM为10080的构造，并证明没有更小的LCM可以实现。证明采用逐级过滤论证：先对7的倍数应用倒数求和过滤器，再应用除数完备整数规划过滤器，然后应用更强的部分和过滤器，最后对少数遗留困难案例用Gurobi完全计算验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Klein关于最小模数为7的不同覆盖系统最小公倍数的最小性猜想，即确定该最小公倍数的精确下界。

Method: 采用逐级过滤论证方法：(1) 倒数求和过滤器；(2) 除数完备整数规划过滤器；(3) 更强的部分和过滤器；最后对剩余困难案例使用Gurobi进行完全计算验证。

Result: 构造了一个LCM为10080的覆盖系统，并证明不存在LCM小于10080的系统，从而将Klein的界从15120改进到10080。

Conclusion: 最小模数为7的不同覆盖系统的最小可能最小公倍数为10080，推翻了Klein的猜想并给出了最优值。

Abstract: We determine the minimum possible least common multiple of a distinct covering system whose minimum modulus is $7$. Klein previously constructed such a system with least common multiple $15120$ and conjectured that this value was minimal. We give a construction with least common multiple $10080$, and we prove that no smaller least common multiple can occur. The proof is organized as a successive filtering argument. Starting from the possible multiples of $7$ below $10080$, we first apply a reciprocal-sum filter, then a divisor-completed integer-programming filter, then a stronger partial-sum filter. The few remaining hard cases are finally certified by complete Gurobi computations.

</details>


### [115] [On canonical roots of fractional ideals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19135)
*Daniel M. H. van Gent*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出一个多项式时间算法，计算任意序R的分式理想的根，不要求R是Dedekind整环，并确保算法的函子性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数域的最大序通常无法在多项式时间内得到，因此需要在不假设R为Dedekind整环的情况下计算分式理想的根，同时确保算法的输出具有良好定义和函子性。

Method: 推广了Dade–Taussky–Zassenhaus以及Ge和Buchmann–Eisenbrand的结果，设计了一个满足函子性约束的多项式时间算法。

Result: 得到了一个多项式时间算法，能够计算任意序R的分式理想的根，且输出在函子意义下唯一确定。

Conclusion: 在非Dedekind整环的设定下，仍可构造具有函子性的理想根计算算法，推广了经典结论。

Abstract: We give an algorithm to compute in polynomial time the roots of a fractional ideal of an order $R$. We take care not to assume $R$ is Dedekind, since the maximal order of a number field is generally inaccessible in polynomial time. Consequently, the output of such an algorithm is no longer uniquely defined. For it to be a satisfying algorithm we additionally require it be functorial, i.e., isomorphisms on the inputs should induce isomorphisms on the outputs. To adhere to these two constraints, we generalize results from Dade--Taussky--Zassenhaus, and Ge and Buchmann--Eisenbrand.

</details>


### [116] [On the $\mathcal{A}$-transcendence of a Champernowne-type constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19337)
*Shin-ichiro Seki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对任意非零整系数多项式 f(x)，存在无穷多个正整数 n 使得第 n 个素数 p_n 不整除 f(n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数索引与多项式值之间的整除关系，探索素数序列与整系数多项式相互作用的深层数论性质。

Method: 采用数论中素数分布与多项式整除性的经典论证方法，通过构造性证明或反证法来展示无穷多反例的存在性。

Result: 证明了对于任意非零整系数多项式 f(x)，均存在无穷多个正整数 n 使得 p_n ∤ f(n)。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了素数序列与多项式值之间一种普遍的互素性质，丰富了素数分布与整除理论的研究。

Abstract: Let $p_n$ denote the $n$-th prime. We prove that for every nonzero polynomial $f(x)\in\mathbb{Z}[x]$, there exist infinitely many positive integers $n$ such that $p_n\nmid f(n)$.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Heaps of modules: homological aspects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18328)
*Yongduo Wang,Chenyu Wang,Jian He,Dejun Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在T-模堆范畴中定义了投射对象和Gorenstein投射对象，证明了堆的投射性与Gorenstein投射性等价于其对应R(T)-模的相应性质，给出了BP Gorenstein投射维数的函子描述，并证明了单值Truss T是Gorenstein truss当且仅当R(T)是Iwanaga-Gorenstein环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的模论中投射对象和Gorenstein投射对象的概念推广到T-模堆范畴中，建立堆范畴与模范畴之间的对应关系，并刻画Gorenstein truss的环论特征。

Method: 首先在T-模堆范畴中定义投射对象和Gorenstein投射对象；然后通过函子$\mathcal{G}_{e}$建立堆与R(T)-模之间的联系，证明堆的（Gorenstein）投射性等价于其像模的相应性质；最后利用函子描述给出BP Gorenstein投射维数，并证明Gorenstein truss与Iwanaga-Gorenstein环的等价关系。

Result: 证明了T-模堆P是投射的当且仅当对所有e_p∈P，$\mathcal{G}_{e_p}(P)$是投射R(T)-模；M是BP Gorenstein投射当且仅当对所有e_m∈M，$\mathcal{G}_{e_m}(M)$是Gorenstein投射R(T)-模；单值Truss T是Gorenstein truss当且仅当R(T)是Iwanaga-Gorenstein环。

Conclusion: 成功将投射和Gorenstein投射概念从模范畴推广到堆范畴，揭示了堆的性质完全由其对应的模决定，并建立了Gorenstein truss与经典环论中Iwanaga-Gorenstein环之间的等价关系。

Abstract: The definitions of projective objects and Gorenstein projective objects in the category of heaps of $T$-modules are posed, where $T$ is a truss. It is shown that a heap of $T$-modules $P$ is projective if and only if $\mathcal{G}_{e_p}(P)$ is a projective $R(T)$-module for all $e_p\in P$ and a heap of $T$-modules $M$ is BP Gorenstein projective if and only if $\mathcal{G}_{e_m}(M)$ is a Gorenstein projective $R(T)$-module for all $e_m\in M$. Moreover, we give a functorial description of the BP Gorenstein projective dimension. Finally, it is also proven that a unital truss $T$ is a Gorenstein truss if and only if $R(T)$ is an Iwanaga-Gorenstein ring.

</details>


### [118] [Cyclic covers and non-orbit 3-representation-finite symmetric algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18497)
*Tor Kringeland*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在特征零的代数闭域上，本文构造了两个3-表示有限但非轨道代数的对称代数：一个是36维的Q(3A)²₂提升，另一个是84维的3-球面带权曲面代数。通过引入幂等导子和箭图特征群，作者证明任何连通对称代数的轨道表示都要求代数或其循环覆盖具有半维平方零分次结构，而所构造代数无法满足此条件，从而否定了Darpö和Iyama提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Darpö和Iyama提出的问题：是否存在3-表示有限的对称代数，它不是任何有限维代数（不限整体维数）的轨道代数？此前已知的3-表示有限对称代数均为轨道代数，本文希望构造反例。

Method: 1. 构造36维代数A，它是特征2中Q(3A)²₂（Böhmler-Marczinzik证明的3-表示有限）在特征零的提升，并显式构造其3-丛倾斜模。2. 建立理论工具：证明连通对称代数的任何轨道表示都迫使代数或其连通循环覆盖具有半维平方零分次结构；引入幂等导子检测该分次结构，并用有限箭图特征群约束可能的覆盖。3. 逐一排除A的所有双覆盖候选（仅剩三个），用导子阻碍证明其不满足条件。4. 同样方法处理84维3-球面带权曲面代数。

Result: 成功构造了两个3-表示有限但非轨道代数的对称代数：36维的代数A和84维的3-球面带权曲面代数。证明它们无法写成任何有限维代数（不限整体维数）的轨道代数。

Conclusion: 存在3-表示有限的对称代数不是轨道代数，回答了Darpö和Iyama的公开问题。幂等导子和箭图特征群方法是判断轨道代数表示的有效工具。

Abstract: Over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero, we exhibit two symmetric algebras that are 3-representation-finite but are not orbit algebras of repetitive categories. The first is a 36-dimensional characteristic-zero lift $A$ of $Q(3A)^2_2$, the quaternion-type algebra that Böhmler and Marczinzik proved 3-representation-finite in characteristic 2; we construct an explicit 3-cluster-tilting module. We show that any orbit presentation of a connected symmetric algebra forces the algebra, or a connected cyclic cover of it, to admit a half-dimensional square-zero grading. Such gradings are detected by idempotent derivations, and a finite group of arrow characters constrains the possible covers. For $A$, only three double-cover candidates remain and the derivation obstruction excludes all of them. Thus $A$ is not an orbit algebra of any finite-dimensional algebra, regardless of global dimension, answering a question of Darpö and Iyama. The 84-dimensional 3-spherical weighted surface algebra is likewise 3-representation-finite and not an orbit algebra.

</details>


### [119] [A Counterexample to Wehlau's Conjecture on Noether Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18585)
*Muhammad Fazeel Anwar*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Wehlau关于Noether数在子模包含下单调性的猜想在特征2下不成立。通过二面体群D8在5维和6维模上的具体构造，给出了反例：β(k[U]^{D8})=6 > 5=β(k[V]^{D8})，其中U⊆V。该构造在任意特征2的域上有效，并通过膨胀给出所有以D8为商群的有限群的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Wehlau提出的猜想：对于有限群G的G-子模包含U⊆V，对应的Noether数是否满足β(k[U]^G) ≤ β(k[V]^G)。该猜想若成立，将为不变量环的生成元次数提供重要的单调性性质。

Method: 在特征2的域上，选取二面体群D8，构造一个具体的5维子模U嵌入6维模V，计算并比较两者不变式环的Noether数。通过具体的不变量计算证明β(k[U]^{D8})=6 > β(k[V]^{D8})=5。

Result: 成功构造出反例：存在U⊆V使得β(k[U]^G) > β(k[V]^G)，精确值为6 > 5。该构造在F2上定义，适用于任意特征2的域，并通过膨胀推广到所有以D8为商群的有限群。

Conclusion: Wehlau猜想在特征2下不成立。Noether数在子模包含下不具单调性，这一反例揭示了有限群不变量环的生成元次数与模结构之间更复杂的关联。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group, let $V$ be a finite-dimensional $G$-module over a field $k$, and let $U$ be a $G$-submodule of $V$. Wehlau conjectured that the corresponding Noether numbers satisfy $β\bigl(k[U]^G\bigr)\leq β\bigl(k[V]^G\bigr)$. We disprove this conjecture in characteristic $2$. For the dihedral group $D_8$, we construct an inclusion $U\subseteq V$, with $\dim_kU=5$ and $\dim_kV=6$, such that $β\bigl(k[U]^{D_8}\bigr)=6>5=β\bigl(k[V]^{D_8}\bigr)$. The construction is defined over $\mathbb F_2$ and remains valid over every field of characteristic $2$. By inflation, it yields counterexamples for every finite group admitting $D_8$ as a quotient.

</details>


### [120] [Hopf $2$-cocycles for certain affine algebraic reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18599)
*Shlomo Gelaki*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文分类了仿射代数约化群G（其单位连通分支为环面T）上的（极小）Hopf 2-上循环，通过研究无限半单张量范畴Rep(T)^K上的有限不可分解半单模范畴，证明了Rep(G)上秩1的模范畴对应的纤维函子是经典的（保持维数），从而与Hopf 2-上循环一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受仿射代数约化群G上Hopf 2-上循环分类这一开放问题驱动，特别源于EG1中关于极小Hopf 2-上循环的提问（Question 7.2）。

Method: 利用ENO中Proposition 5.4，研究无限半单张量范畴Rep(T)^K上的有限不可分解半单模范畴分类，其中K=G/T，该张量范畴等价于Rep(G)。

Result: 分类了Rep(G)上秩1的模范畴，并证明对应的纤维函子是经典的（保持维数），从而与Hopf 2-上循环一一对应。

Conclusion: 完成了单位连通分支为环面T的仿射代数约化群G上（极小）Hopf 2-上循环的分类。

Abstract: Motivated by the open problem of classifying Hopf $2$-cocycles for affine algebraic reductive groups $G$ over $\mathbb{C}$, in particular by \cite[Question 7.2]{EG1} which concerns minimal Hopf $2$-cocycles, we classify (minimal) Hopf $2$-cocycles for affine algebraic reductive groups $G$ whose connected component of the identity is a torus $T$. To achieve this we utilize \cite[Proposition 5.4]{ENO} in our situation to classify finite indecomposable semisimple module categories over the infinite semisimple equivariantization tensor category $\Rep(T)^K\simeq \Rep(G)$, where $K:=G/T$. We then use it to classify the module categories over $\Rep(G)$ of rank $1$, and show that the corresponding fiber functors on $\Rep(G)$ are classical (that is, preserve dimensions), which implies that they are in bijection with Hopf $2$-cocycles for $G$.

</details>


### [121] [On the absolute convergence of the spectral side of the twisted trace formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18870)
*Werner Müller,Satoshi Wakatsuki*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了约化代数群扭曲迹公式谱侧的绝对收敛性，将Finis–Lapid–Müller的绝对收敛定理推广到扭曲设定，并给出了此前专家间已知但未曾在文献中出现的详细证明。该结果应用于极限重数问题和GL_n自对偶自守表示的Weyl律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决扭曲迹公式谱侧绝对收敛性的严格证明缺失问题，该结论虽为专家所知但未有文献记录，同时为极限重数问题和自对偶自守表示的Weyl律提供理论支撑。

Method: 将Finis–Lapid–Müller关于非扭曲情形下迹公式谱侧绝对收敛的证明方法推广至扭曲设定，并给出完整的详细证明过程。

Result: 成功建立了约化代数群扭曲迹公式谱侧的绝对收敛性。

Conclusion: 扭曲迹公式谱侧的绝对收敛定理得以严格证明，填补了文献空白，并为后续极限重数问题和自对偶自守表示Weyl律的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: We establish the absolute convergence of the spectral side of the twisted trace formula for reductive algebraic groups. More precisely, we extend the absolute convergence theorem of the spectral side due to Finis--Lapid--Müller to the twisted setting. This paper provides a detailed proof of the absolute convergence theorem, whose proof is known to experts but does not seem to have appeared in the literature. The resulting formulas are motivated by applications to limit multiplicity problems and to Weyl laws for self-dual automorphic representations of $\mathrm{GL}_n$.

</details>


### [122] [Generic polar divisors and flag residues for root-system zeta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18945)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可约晶体根系Φ的未扭曲Komori-Matsumoto-Tsumura（KMT）zeta函数，证明了每个适当支撑超平面H_{S,ℓ}在一般点处是真正极除子，而精确齐次性只保留未偏移的全支撑除子。在最大支撑奇妙模型上，边界贡献由严格装饰旗索引。推导了递归旗留数、分量质量gamma因子以及横截面上Laurent系数的完备公式：极点阶等于非零聚合系数的最大阶，而非单个旗的最大阶。一般公式复现了A₂、A₃、C₂、G₂等经典结果，以及B₃和C₃的载体几何和低秩分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统地理解KMT zeta函数的极点结构和留数性质，建立一般根系上多重zeta函数的统一解析延拓框架，并推广已知的Witten zeta函数和Euler-Zagier型留数公式。

Method: 利用超平面配置的极除子分析和最大支撑奇妙模型（maximal support wonderful model）的边界分解技术，通过递归旗留数和分量质量gamma因子计算Laurent系数，结合聚合系数求和确定极点阶。

Result: 证明了H_{S,ℓ}在一般点为真正极除子，精确齐次性仅保留全支撑除子；推导了横截面上Laurent系数的完备公式，极点阶由聚合系数非零的最大阶决定；复现了A₂、A₃、C₂、G₂的经典结果，以及B₃和C₃的载体几何和低秩分解，识别了B₃在s=1/8的三项抵消，证明了负半整数是唯一可能的双极点位置。

Conclusion: 本文建立了KMT zeta函数在一般根系上的极点结构和留数理论，统一并推广了Witten zeta函数、Euler-Zagier留数公式及多种经典结果，为多重zeta函数的研究提供了系统的代数组合工具。

Abstract: Let $Φ$ be an irreducible crystallographic root system and let $Z_Φ(\mathbf{s})$ be the untwisted Komori-Matsumoto-Tsumura zeta function of $Φ$, with one complex exponent for each positive coroot; its diagonal specialization is the single-variable Witten zeta function. For a nonempty set $S$ of simple nodes and an integer $\ell\ge 0$, let $H_{S,\ell}$ be the hyperplane on which the exponents of the roots meeting $S$ sum to $|S|-\ell$. We prove that every proper-support hyperplane $H_{S,\ell}$ is a genuine polar divisor at a generic point, while exact homogeneity leaves only the unshifted full-support divisor; each generic residue is an explicit finite Taylor-jet sum of projective periods and polynomially weighted zeta functions of complementary subsystems. On the maximal support wonderful model, boundary contributions are indexed by strict decorated flags. We derive recursive flag residues, component-mass gamma factors, and an incidence-complete formula for the Laurent coefficients on any transverse slice: the pole order equals the largest order with nonzero aggregate coefficient, not the largest order of an individual flag. The general formulas recover the classical $A_2$ and $A_3$ singular data, Zhao's Euler-Zagier residue formulas and the Akiyama-Egami-Tanigawa lists, and the $C_2$ and $G_2$ residue functions. For $B_3$ and $C_3$ we derive the carrier geometry and lower-rank factorizations of the positive diagonal residues, identify the three-term cancellation at $s=1/8$, and show that negative half-integers are the only possible double-pole locations, recovering the known $B_3$ double coefficient at $s=-1/2$.

</details>


### [123] [Archimedean Modular Symbols for Rankin-Selberg Convolutions of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ and $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19133)
*Yubo Jin,Zeyu Tian*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文显式计算了 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ 和 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$ 的 Rankin-Selberg 卷积的阿基米德模符号，针对所有一般的上同调表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Rankin-Selberg 卷积在阿基米德局部域的模符号，以理解自守形式与 L-函数在无穷远点的算术性质。

Method: 利用显式计算方法，针对所有一般的上同调表示，对 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ 和 $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$ 两类情形进行阿基米德模符号的精确计算。

Result: 成功获得了上述两类 Rankin-Selberg 卷积的阿基米德模符号的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解一般线性群上 Rankin-Selberg L-函数在无穷远点的算术性质提供了关键工具，补充了非阿基米德情形的相关理论。

Abstract: We explicit calculate the archimedean modular symbol for Rankin-Selberg convolutions of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ and $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})\times\mathrm{GL}_{n+1}(\mathbb{C})$, for all generic cohomological representations.

</details>
